"""The starting model: a small dense GPT-2-style language model.

Four transformer blocks (width 256, 4 heads, causal attention with RoPE on
queries and keys, GELU-tanh MLP of width 896), each used once per token. The
model returns the final hidden states; the harness turns them into logits
with the token embedding table (tied output head). 2,896,928 unique weights besides
the 8192 x 256 embedding (not counted), about 12M FLOPs per token.

Initialisation: weights N(0, 0.02), biases 0, and every block's output
projections (attention and MLP c_proj) N(0, 0.02 / sqrt(2 * n_layers)).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass

import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch import nn


@dataclass
class ModelConfig:
    vocab_size: int = 8192
    d_model: int = 256
    n_heads: int = 4
    n_layers: int = 4
    mlp_width: int = 896
    rope_theta: float = 10_000.0
    layer_norm_eps: float = 1e-5


class Attention(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.n_heads = cfg.n_heads
        self.head_dim = cfg.d_model // cfg.n_heads
        self.c_attn = nn.Linear(cfg.d_model, 3 * cfg.d_model)
        self.c_proj = nn.Linear(cfg.d_model, cfg.d_model)

    def forward(self, x, cos, sin):
        batch, length, width = x.shape
        q, k, v = self.c_attn(x).split(width, dim=2)
        q, k, v = (t.view(batch, length, self.n_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2) for t in (q, k, v))
        q, k = q * cos + _rotate_half(q) * sin, k * cos + _rotate_half(k) * sin
        out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
        return self.c_proj(out.transpose(1, 2).reshape(batch, length, width))


class MLP(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.c_fc = nn.Linear(cfg.d_model, cfg.mlp_width)
        self.c_proj = nn.Linear(cfg.mlp_width, cfg.d_model)

    def forward(self, x):
        return self.c_proj(F.gelu(self.c_fc(x), approximate="tanh"))


class Block(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.ln_1 = nn.LayerNorm(cfg.d_model, eps=cfg.layer_norm_eps)
        self.attn = Attention(cfg)
        self.ln_2 = nn.LayerNorm(cfg.d_model, eps=cfg.layer_norm_eps)
        self.mlp = MLP(cfg)

    def forward(self, x, cos, sin):
        x = x + self.attn(self.ln_1(x), cos, sin)
        return x + self.mlp(self.ln_2(x))


def _rotate_half(x):
    first, second = x.chunk(2, dim=-1)
    return torch.cat((-second, first), dim=-1)


class GPT(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.embed = nn.Embedding(cfg.vocab_size, cfg.d_model)
        self.blocks = nn.ModuleList(Block(cfg) for _ in range(cfg.n_layers))
        self.ln_f = nn.LayerNorm(cfg.d_model, eps=cfg.layer_norm_eps)
        head_dim = cfg.d_model // cfg.n_heads
        inv_freq = 1.0 / cfg.rope_theta ** (torch.arange(0, head_dim, 2, dtype=torch.float32) / head_dim)
        self.register_buffer("inv_freq", inv_freq, persistent=False)
        nn.init.normal_(self.embed.weight, std=0.02)
        for module in self.blocks.modules():
            if isinstance(module, nn.Linear):
                nn.init.normal_(module.weight, std=0.02)
                nn.init.zeros_(module.bias)
        for block in self.blocks:
            nn.init.normal_(block.attn.c_proj.weight, std=0.02 / math.sqrt(2 * cfg.n_layers))
            nn.init.normal_(block.mlp.c_proj.weight, std=0.02 / math.sqrt(2 * cfg.n_layers))
        self.stack = self.apply_blocks  # train.py replaces this with a compiled version

    def apply_blocks(self, h, cos, sin):
        for block in self.blocks:
            h = block(h, cos, sin)
        return h

    def forward(self, input_ids):
        length = input_ids.shape[1]
        positions = torch.arange(length, device=input_ids.device, dtype=torch.float32)
        angles = torch.outer(positions, self.inv_freq)
        angles = torch.cat((angles, angles), dim=-1)[None, None]
        h = self.stack(self.embed(input_ids), angles.cos(), angles.sin())
        return self.ln_f(h)  # the harness applies the tied output head


def build_model(config: dict) -> GPT:
    """config is config.json plus {"vocab_size": ...} from the harness."""

    return GPT(ModelConfig(vocab_size=config["vocab_size"], **config.get("model", {})))
"""Starting training recipe: plain next-token loss.

Random 512-token windows, AdamW (betas 0.9, 0.95; weight decay on matrices
only), bf16 autocast, gradient clipping at 1, a linear warmup over the first
5% of the budget and a cosine decay to 10% of the learning rate over the
rest. Training stops when the budget is spent.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math

import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F


def train(ctx):
    cfg = ctx.config["train"]
    model, budget = ctx.model, ctx.budget
    if cfg["compile"] and ctx.device.type == "cuda":
        model.inner.body.stack = torch.compile(model.inner.body.apply_blocks)
    params = list(model.parameters())
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW([{"params": [p for p in params if p.dim() >= 2], "weight_decay": cfg["weight_decay"]},
                                   {"params": [p for p in params if p.dim() < 2], "weight_decay": 0.0}],
                                  lr=cfg["learning_rate"], betas=(0.9, 0.95), fused=ctx.device.type == "cuda")

    def lr_at(progress: float) -> float:
        lr, warmup = cfg["learning_rate"], cfg["warmup"]
        if progress < warmup:
            return lr * max(progress, 1e-4) / warmup
        p = (progress - warmup) / (1 - warmup)
        return lr * (0.1 + 0.9 * 0.5 * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * min(1.0, p))))

    update = 0
    model.train()
    while not budget.exhausted:
        for group in optimizer.param_groups:
            group["lr"] = lr_at(budget.fraction_used)
        total = 0.0
        for _ in range(cfg["accumulation"]):
            batch = ctx.data.sample(cfg["micro_batch"])
            with torch.autocast(ctx.device.type, dtype=torch.bfloat16):
                logits = model(batch.tokens)
            loss = F.cross_entropy(logits.float().flatten(0, 1), batch.targets.flatten())
            (loss / cfg["accumulation"]).backward()
            total += loss.item() / cfg["accumulation"]
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params, 1.0)
        optimizer.step()
        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        update += 1
        if not math.isfinite(total):
            raise FloatingPointError(f"loss is {total} at update {update}")
        if update % cfg["log_every"] == 0:
            ctx.log({"update": update, "loss": total, "lr": optimizer.param_groups[0]["lr"]})

Four transformer blocks (width 256, 4 heads, causal attention with RoPE on
queries and keys, GELU-tanh MLP of width 896), each used once per token. The

projections (attention and MLP c_proj) N(0, 0.02 / sqrt(2 * n_layers)).

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass

import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch import nn


@dataclass
class ModelConfig:
    vocab_size: int = 8192
    d_model: int = 256
    n_heads: int = 4
    n_layers: int = 4
    mlp_width: int = 896

        self.c_proj = nn.Linear(cfg.d_model, cfg.d_model)

        q, k, v = self.c_attn(x).split(width, dim=2)

        q, k = q * cos + _rotate_half(q) * sin, k * cos + _rotate_half(k) * sin

        return self.c_proj(out.transpose(1, 2).reshape(batch, length, width))

        super().__init__()

        self.c_proj = nn.Linear(cfg.mlp_width, cfg.d_model)

    def forward(self, x):
        return self.c_proj(F.gelu(self.c_fc(x), approximate="tanh"))

        super().__init__()

        self.attn = Attention(cfg)
        self.ln_2 = nn.LayerNorm(cfg.d_model, eps=cfg.layer_norm_eps)
        self.mlp = MLP(cfg)

    def forward(self, x, cos, sin):
        x = x + self.attn(self.ln_1(x), cos, sin)
        return x + self.mlp(self.ln_2(x))

    first, second = x.chunk(2, dim=-1)
    return torch.cat((-second, first), dim=-1)

        self.embed = nn.Embedding(cfg.vocab_size, cfg.d_model)
        self.blocks = nn.ModuleList(Block(cfg) for _ in range(cfg.n_layers))
        self.ln_f = nn.LayerNorm(cfg.d_model, eps=cfg.layer_norm_eps)
        head_dim = cfg.d_model // cfg.n_heads
        inv_freq = 1.0 / cfg.rope_theta ** (torch.arange(0, head_dim, 2, dtype=torch.float32) / head_dim)
        self.register_buffer("inv_freq", inv_freq, persistent=False)
        nn.init.normal_(self.embed.weight, std=0.02)
        for module in self.blocks.modules():
            if isinstance(module, nn.Linear):
                nn.init.normal_(module.weight, std=0.02)
                nn.init.zeros_(module.bias)
        for block in self.blocks:
            nn.init.normal_(block.attn.c_proj.weight, std=0.02 / math.sqrt(2 * cfg.n_layers))
            nn.init.normal_(block.mlp.c_proj.weight, std=0.02 / math.sqrt(2 * cfg.n_layers))

    def apply_blocks(self, h, cos, sin):
        for block in self.blocks:
            h = block(h, cos, sin)
        return h

"""What a submission provides.

submission/model.py defines

    def build_model(config: dict) -> nn.Module

where config is submission/config.json plus {"vocab_size": ...}. The model
maps token ids to hidden states, and must have its token embedding table as
the attribute embed = nn.Embedding(vocab_size, width):

    hidden = model(input_ids)              # [B, L] -> [B, L, width]

The harness owns the output layer: it wraps the model in
harness.OutputHead, which computes the next-token logits as

    logits = hidden @ model.embed.weight.T  # [B, L, vocab]

so every prediction goes through that table. Everything below calls the
wrapped model, which returns logits. Position i's logits may depend only on
tokens 0..i. Evaluation calls
model(input_ids) in eval mode with no other arguments; that call must stay
within task.INFERENCE_FLOP_CAP FLOPs per token. Any architecture is allowed
(dense, shared or looped blocks, anything else) within task.WEIGHT_CAP
unique weights.

In training, train.py may pass keyword arguments (say, a number of loops):
model(input_ids, loops=12). How much compute a call costs may depend only on
those arguments, the training/eval mode and the input's shape, never on
random draws inside the model or on the batch size: the harness measures
each kind of call once and charges every later call of that kind its tokens
times that cost.

submission/train.py defines

    def train(ctx: TrainContext) -> None

which trains ctx.model (a metered wrapper around OutputHead(build_model(config)),
see harness.MeteredModel; ctx.model(input_ids) returns logits, and the
submission's own module is ctx.model.inner.body) until the budget runs out: task.TRAIN_TOKENS tokens
through the model and task.TRAIN_FLOPS forward FLOPs, whichever comes first.
A call after that raises BudgetExhausted, which ends training normally. The
harness saves the model's weights when train returns or the budget is spent.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Callable

import torch


class BudgetExhausted(RuntimeError):
    """Raised by ctx.model when it is called after the training budget is spent; ends training normally."""


@dataclass
class Batch:
    tokens: torch.Tensor  # [B, L] input token ids
    targets: torch.Tensor  # [B, L] the next token at each position


@dataclass
class TrainContext:
    model: Any  # harness.MeteredModel
    budget: Any  # harness.Budget: .exhausted, .fraction_used, .used_tokens, .used_flops
    data: Any  # harness.TokenStream: .sample(batch_size, seq_len=task.SEQ_LEN) -> Batch
    config: dict  # submission/config.json
    seed: int
    device: torch.device
    log: Callable[[dict], None]  # records a dict of numbers in the run's train.json (and prints it)

"""What a submission provides.

submission/model.py defines

    def build_model(config: dict) -> nn.Module

where config is submission/config.json plus {"vocab_size": ...}. The model
maps token ids to hidden states, and must have its token embedding table as

#3 Bashcat /opt/lmenv-src/lmenv/harness.py
cat /opt/lmenv-src/lmenv/harness.py
"""The harness around a submission: weight count, FLOP metering, data, training run, evaluation.

Compute is counted in FLOPs with torch's FlopCounterMode (uncompiled; on the
CPU attention is swapped for an explicit matmul version so it is counted).
The submission's model returns hidden states; build() wraps it in
OutputHead, which turns them into logits with the model's token embedding
table, so every model here maps ids to logits. train.py sees the model only
through MeteredModel. Each kind of call (mode,
input length, keyword arguments) is measured once on a one-row batch, with
autograd on as in training; every call then charges its tokens and tokens x
that cost to the budget. A call after the budget is spent raises
BudgetExhausted, which ends training normally. These checks run inside the
submission's process and catch honest mistakes; the grader re-checks the
model from outside (checks.py).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import copy
import importlib.util
import json
import math
import sys
import time
import traceback
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch import nn
from torch.utils.flop_counter import FlopCounterMode

from lmenv import task
from lmenv.api import Batch, BudgetExhausted, TrainContext


@dataclass
class Budget:
    tokens: float = task.TRAIN_TOKENS
    flops: float = task.TRAIN_FLOPS
    used_tokens: float = 0.0
    used_flops: float = 0.0
    started: float = field(default_factory=time.monotonic)

    def charge(self, tokens: float, flops: float) -> None:
        if self.exhausted:
            raise BudgetExhausted(self.describe())
        self.used_tokens += float(tokens)
        self.used_flops += float(flops)

    @property
    def elapsed_s(self) -> float:
        return time.monotonic() - self.started

    @property
    def exhausted(self) -> bool:
        return self.fraction_used >= 1.0

    @property
    def fraction_used(self) -> float:
        return max(self.used_tokens / self.tokens, self.used_flops / self.flops)

    def describe(self) -> str:
        return (f"{self.used_tokens:.3g}/{self.tokens:.3g} tokens, {self.used_flops:.3g}/{self.flops:.3g} FLOPs, "
                f"{self.elapsed_s:.0f} s")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- measurements

@contextlib.contextmanager
def countable_attention(device: torch.device):
    """On the CPU, FlopCounterMode misses fused attention: count an explicit version instead."""

    if device.type != "cpu":
        yield
        return
    original = F.scaled_dot_product_attention

    def explicit(q, k, v, attn_mask=None, dropout_p=0.0, is_causal=False, scale=None, **_):
        scores = q @ k.transpose(-2, -1) * (scale or 1.0 / math.sqrt(q.shape[-1]))
        if is_causal:
            length = q.shape[-2]
            scores = scores.masked_fill(~torch.ones(length, length, dtype=torch.bool).tril(), float("-inf"))
        if attn_mask is not None:
            scores = scores.masked_fill(~attn_mask, float("-inf")) if attn_mask.dtype == torch.bool else scores + attn_mask
        return torch.softmax(scores, dim=-1) @ v

    F.scaled_dot_product_attention = explicit
    try:
        yield
    finally:
        F.scaled_dot_product_attention = original


def flops_per_token(model: nn.Module, ids: torch.Tensor, kwargs: dict | None = None, training: bool = False,
                    grad: bool = False) -> float:
    """Forward FLOPs per token of model(ids, **kwargs) in the given mode, uncompiled."""

    was_training = model.training
    model.train(training)
    devices = [ids.device] if ids.device.type == "cuda" else []
    # force_eager runs compiled functions uncompiled just for this call. (Setting
    # torch._dynamo.config.disable instead marks a compiled function that is first
    # called here as never to be compiled, so training ran uncompiled.)
    try:
        with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=devices), torch.set_grad_enabled(grad), countable_attention(ids.device), \
                torch.compiler.set_stance("force_eager"):
            with FlopCounterMode(display=False) as counter:
                model(ids, **(kwargs or {}))
        return counter.get_total_flops() / ids.numel()
    finally:
        model.train(was_training)


def unique_weights(model: nn.Module, vocab_size: int) -> dict:
    """Unique floating-point weights (parameters and buffers, one count per storage), minus one exempt table.

    The exempt table is the largest stored tensor of shape (vocab, width) with
    width <= EXEMPT_TABLE_WIDTH: the token embedding, or the head tied to it.
    Integer and boolean buffers (masks, indices) are not weights and are not counted.
    """

    storages: dict[int, tuple[int, tuple]] = {}
    for tensor in [*model.parameters(), *model.buffers()]:
        if not (tensor.is_floating_point() or tensor.is_complex()) or tensor.numel() == 0:
            continue
        storage = tensor.untyped_storage()
        elements = storage.nbytes() // tensor.element_size()
        key = storage.data_ptr()
        if key not in storages or storages[key][0] < elements:
            storages[key] = (elements, tuple(tensor.shape))
    total = sum(n for n, _ in storages.values())
    tables = [n for n, shape in storages.values()
              if len(shape) == 2 and shape[0] == vocab_size and shape[1] <= task.EXEMPT_TABLE_WIDTH and n == shape[0] * shape[1]]
    exempt = max(tables, default=0)
    return {"total": total, "exempt": exempt, "counted": total - exempt}


@torch.no_grad()
def interface_failures(model: nn.Module, vocab_size: int, device, perturb: bool = True) -> list[str]:
    """model(ids) gives [B, L, vocab] logits, and position i depends only on tokens 0..i.

    With ``perturb``, the test runs on a copy with small noise added to every
    weight: a freshly initialised model can hide a leak behind zero-initialised
    projections or gates that training would open.
    """

    if perturb:
        model = copy.deepcopy(model)
        noise = torch.Generator(device="cpu").manual_seed(1)
        for p in model.parameters():
            if p.is_floating_point():
                p.add_(0.02 * torch.randn(p.shape, generator=noise).to(p.device, p.dtype))
    was_training = model.training
    model.eval()
    gen = torch.Generator().manual_seed(0)
    ids = torch.randint(0, vocab_size, (2, 64), generator=gen).to(device)
    changed = ids.clone()
    changed[:, 40:] = torch.randint(0, vocab_size, (2, 24), generator=gen).to(device)
    try:
        logits = model(ids).float()
        if tuple(logits.shape) != (2, 64, vocab_size):
            return [f"the model's output has shape {tuple(logits.shape)}, expected (batch, length, vocab)"]
        moved = (model(changed).float()[:, :40] - logits[:, :40]).abs().max().item()
        if moved > 1e-3:
            return [f"not causal: changing tokens 40.. moves the logits of earlier positions by {moved:.3g}"]
    except Exception as error:  # the submission's code can fail in any way
        return [f"the model cannot be run: {type(error).__name__}: {error}"[:500]]
    finally:
        model.train(was_training)
    return []


def cap_failures(model: nn.Module, vocab_size: int, device) -> tuple[dict, list[str]]:
    """Unique weights and inference FLOPs per token against the caps."""

    weights = unique_weights(model, vocab_size)
    ids = torch.randint(0, vocab_size, (1, task.SEQ_LEN), generator=torch.Generator().manual_seed(0)).to(device)
    flops = flops_per_token(model, ids)
    failures = []
    if weights["counted"] > task.WEIGHT_CAP:
        failures.append(f"{weights['counted']:,} unique weights (cap {task.WEIGHT_CAP:,}; one embedding table of "
                        f"{weights['exempt']:,} not counted)")
    if flops > task.INFERENCE_FLOP_CAP:
        failures.append(f"inference costs {flops / 1e6:.1f}M FLOPs per token (cap {task.INFERENCE_FLOP_CAP / 1e6:.0f}M)")
    return {"weights": weights, "inference_flops_per_token": flops}, failures


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- the metered model

def _call_key(training: bool, ids: torch.Tensor, kwargs: dict) -> tuple:
    for name, value in kwargs.items():
        if not isinstance(value, (bool, int, float, str, type(None))):
            raise TypeError(f"model keyword argument {name!r} must be a number, string, bool or None, "
                            f"not {type(value).__name__}")
    return (training, ids.dim(), ids.shape[-1], tuple(sorted(kwargs.items())))


class MeteredModel(nn.Module):
    """ctx.model: calls the submission's model and charges each call to the budget."""

    def __init__(self, model: nn.Module, budget: Budget):
        super().__init__()
        self.inner = model
        self.budget = budget
        self.costs: dict[tuple, float] = {}

    def forward(self, input_ids: torch.Tensor, **kwargs) -> torch.Tensor:
        if self.budget.exhausted:
            raise BudgetExhausted(self.budget.describe())
        key = _call_key(self.inner.training, input_ids, kwargs)
        if key not in self.costs:
            if len(self.costs) >= task.MAX_CALL_KINDS:
                raise RuntimeError(f"more than {task.MAX_CALL_KINDS} kinds of model call")
            self.costs[key] = flops_per_token(self.inner, input_ids[:1], kwargs, training=self.inner.training,
                                              grad=torch.is_grad_enabled())
        tokens = input_ids.numel()
        self.budget.charge(tokens, tokens * self.costs[key])
        return self.inner(input_ids, **kwargs)

    def cost_table(self) -> list[dict]:
        return [{"training": k[0], "length": k[2], "kwargs": dict(k[3]), "flops_per_token": v}
                for k, v in self.costs.items()]


class TokenStream:
    """One pass over the first ``corpus`` training tokens (CORPUS_TOKENS by default), in shuffled order.

    The text is cut into blocks of SEQ_LEN tokens, visited in a random order;
    every token is a target once per pass. A row of seq_len < SEQ_LEN takes the
    next seq_len tokens of the current block (the block's remainder shorter than
    seq_len is skipped). When the blocks run out, a new pass starts in a new order.
    """

    def __init__(self, data: Path, seed: int, corpus: int = task.CORPUS_TOKENS):
        self.tokens = np.memmap(data / "train.bin", dtype=np.uint16, mode="r")[:int(corpus)]
        self.rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.device = torch.device("cpu")
        self.blocks = (len(self.tokens) - 1) // task.SEQ_LEN
        self.order, self.next_block, self.offset = self.rng.permutation(self.blocks), 0, task.SEQ_LEN
        self.passes = 0

    def _row_start(self, seq_len: int) -> int:
        if self.offset + seq_len > task.SEQ_LEN:
            if self.next_block == self.blocks:
                self.order, self.next_block = self.rng.permutation(self.blocks), 0
                self.passes += 1
            self.block, self.next_block, self.offset = self.order[self.next_block], self.next_block + 1, 0
        start = int(self.block) * task.SEQ_LEN + self.offset
        self.offset += seq_len
        return start

    def sample(self, batch_size: int, seq_len: int = task.SEQ_LEN) -> Batch:
        if not 1 <= seq_len <= task.SEQ_LEN:
            raise ValueError(f"seq_len must be in 1..{task.SEQ_LEN}")
        starts = [self._row_start(seq_len) for _ in range(batch_size)]
        rows = np.stack([self.tokens[s:s + seq_len + 1] for s in starts]).astype(np.int64)
        rows = torch.from_numpy(rows).to(self.device, non_blocking=True)
        return Batch(tokens=rows[:, :-1], targets=rows[:, 1:])


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- submission code

def load_module(directory: Path, name: str):
    path = directory / f"{name}.py"
    if not path.exists():
        return None
    if str(directory.resolve()) not in sys.path:
        sys.path.insert(0, str(directory.resolve()))
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, path)
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules[name] = module  # so train.py can "import model"
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def read_config(submission: Path) -> dict:
    path = submission / "config.json"
    return json.loads(path.read_text()) if path.exists() else {}


class OutputHead(nn.Module):
    """The submission's model followed by the harness's output layer.

    The submission's model (``body``) returns hidden states [B, L, width]; the
    logits are those states times its token embedding table, body.embed
    (vocab x width): logits = hidden @ body.embed.weight.T. Every prediction
    therefore goes through the tied table, and nothing can put probability on
    tokens any other way (for example by copying ids from the input).
    """

    def __init__(self, body: nn.Module, vocab_size: int):
        super().__init__()
        embed = getattr(body, "embed", None)
        if not isinstance(embed, nn.Embedding) or embed.num_embeddings != vocab_size:
            raise TypeError(f"the model needs an attribute embed = nn.Embedding({vocab_size}, width); "
                            f"the harness computes the logits as hidden @ embed.weight.T")
        self.body = body

    def forward(self, input_ids: torch.Tensor, **kwargs) -> torch.Tensor:
        hidden = self.body(input_ids, **kwargs)
        if not isinstance(hidden, torch.Tensor):
            raise TypeError(f"the model must return hidden states [batch, length, width], not {type(hidden).__name__}")
        return F.linear(hidden, self.body.embed.weight)


def build(submission: Path, vocab_size: int, device) -> nn.Module:
    """The submission's model inside the harness's output layer (OutputHead)."""

    module = load_module(submission, "model")
    if module is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"{submission}/model.py is missing")
    body = module.build_model({**read_config(submission), "vocab_size": vocab_size})
    return OutputHead(body, vocab_size).to(device)


def load_trained(submission: Path, run: Path, vocab_size: int, device) -> nn.Module:
    model = build(submission, vocab_size, device)
    weights = torch.load(run / "model.pt", map_location=device)
    model.load_state_dict(weights)
    return model.eval()


def saved_state(model: nn.Module) -> dict:
    # torch.compile'd submodules add "_orig_mod." to their names; save the plain names.
    return {k.replace("_orig_mod.", ""): v.detach().cpu() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- training and evaluation

def train_run(submission: Path, data: Path, out: Path, seed: int, device, tokens: float = task.TRAIN_TOKENS,
              log=print, corpus: int = task.CORPUS_TOKENS) -> dict:
    """Train one seed of the submission; writes OUT/model.pt and OUT/train.json and returns the record.

    ``tokens`` below TRAIN_TOKENS scales both budgets down (a quick proxy run).
    ``corpus`` (design experiments only) sets how many training tokens are sampled from.
    """

    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    vocab = json.loads((data / "meta.json").read_text())["vocab_size"]
    record: dict = {"seed": seed, "status": "error", "error": None, "budget_tokens": tokens, "corpus": corpus,
                    "history": []}

    def finish(**fields) -> dict:
        record.update(fields)
        (out / "train.json").write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2))
        return record

    try:
        torch.manual_seed(seed)
        np.random.seed(seed)
        model = build(submission, vocab, device)
        trainer = load_module(submission, "train")
        if trainer is None:
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"{submission}/train.py is missing")
    except Exception as error:
        return finish(error=f"building the model: {type(error).__name__}: {error}"[:2000])
    failures = interface_failures(model, vocab, device)
    if failures:
        return finish(error=failures[0])
    try:
        caps, failures = cap_failures(model, vocab, device)
    except Exception as error:
        return finish(error=f"measuring the model: {type(error).__name__}: {error}"[:2000])
    log(f"{caps['weights']['counted']:,} unique weights (+{caps['weights']['exempt']:,} embedding), "
        f"{caps['inference_flops_per_token'] / 1e6:.1f}M inference FLOPs per token")
    if failures:
        return finish(error="over the caps: " + "; ".join(failures), caps=caps)
    meter = Budget(tokens=tokens, flops=tokens * task.INFERENCE_FLOP_CAP)
    metered = MeteredModel(model, meter)
    stream = TokenStream(data, seed, corpus)
    stream.device = device
    history = record["history"]

    def record_log(entry: dict) -> None:
        entry = {**entry, "tokens": meter.used_tokens, "flops": meter.used_flops, "elapsed_s": round(meter.elapsed_s, 1)}
        history.append(entry)
        log("  ".join(f"{k} {v:.4g}" if isinstance(v, float) else f"{k} {v}" for k, v in entry.items()))

    ctx = TrainContext(model=metered, budget=meter, data=stream, config=read_config(submission), seed=seed,
                       device=device, log=record_log)
    status, error = "complete", None
    started = time.time()
    try:
        trainer.train(ctx)
    except BudgetExhausted:
        pass
    except Exception as e:  # the submission's code can fail in any way
        status, error = "error", f"train.py: {type(e).__name__}: {e}\n{traceback.format_exc()[-1500:]}"
    torch.save(saved_state(model), out / "model.pt")
    return finish(status=status, error=error, caps=caps, used_tokens=meter.used_tokens, used_flops=meter.used_flops,
                  call_costs=metered.cost_table(), train_seconds=time.time() - started,
                  corpus_passes=stream.passes + stream.next_block / stream.blocks)


_TOKEN_BYTES: dict = {}


def token_bytes(tokenizer: Path, eot: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """UTF-8 bytes of each token of our byte-level BPE (the end-of-text token counts as one byte)."""

    key = str(tokenizer)
    if key not in _TOKEN_BYTES:
        vocab = json.loads(tokenizer.read_text())["model"]["vocab"]
        # GPT-2's byte-to-character map: printable bytes stand for themselves, the rest from 256 up.
        printable = [*range(ord("!"), ord("~") + 1), *range(ord("¡"), ord("¬") + 1), *range(ord("®"), ord("ÿ") + 1)]
        to_char = {b: chr(b) for b in printable}
        extra = 0
        for b in range(256):
            if b not in to_char:
                to_char[b] = chr(256 + extra)
                extra += 1
        known = set(to_char.values())
        lengths = np.zeros(max(vocab.values()) + 1, dtype=np.int64)
        for piece, index in vocab.items():
            lengths[index] = len(piece) if all(c in known for c in piece) else len(piece.encode())
        lengths[eot] = 1
        _TOKEN_BYTES[key] = lengths
    return _TOKEN_BYTES[key]


@torch.no_grad()
def evaluate(model: nn.Module, tokens: np.ndarray, device, byte_lengths: np.ndarray | None = None,
             batch: int = 32) -> dict:
    """Mean next-token loss (nats per token) on every full SEQ_LEN window of ``tokens``, and bits per byte."""

    model.eval()
    n = (len(tokens) - 1) // task.SEQ_LEN
    total = torch.zeros((), dtype=torch.float64, device=device)
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    for start in range(0, n, batch):
        stop = min(n, start + batch)
        rows = np.stack([tokens[i * task.SEQ_LEN:(i + 1) * task.SEQ_LEN + 1] for i in range(start, stop)])
        rows = torch.from_numpy(rows.astype(np.int64)).to(device)
        with torch.autocast(device.type, dtype=torch.bfloat16, enabled=device.type == "cuda"):
            logits = model(rows[:, :-1])
        total += F.cross_entropy(logits.float().flatten(0, 1), rows[:, 1:].flatten(), reduction="sum")
    loss = total.item() / (n * task.SEQ_LEN)
    result = {"loss": loss if math.isfinite(loss) else float("inf"), "tokens": n * task.SEQ_LEN}
    if byte_lengths is not None:
        targets = np.asarray(tokens[1:n * task.SEQ_LEN + 1], dtype=np.int64)
        nbytes = int(byte_lengths[targets].sum())
        result["bits_per_byte"] = total.item() / math.log(2) / nbytes
        result["bytes"] = nbytes
    return result

[stdout]
"""The harness around a submission: weight count, FLOP metering, data, training run, evaluation.

Compute is counted in FLOPs with torch's FlopCounterMode (uncompiled; on the
CPU attention is swapped for an explicit matmul version so it is counted).
The submission's model returns hidden states; build() wraps it in
OutputHead, which turns them into logits with the model's token embedding
table, so every model here maps ids to logits. train.py sees the model only
through MeteredModel. Each kind of call (mode,
input length, keyword arguments) is measured once on a one-row batch, with
autograd on as in training; every call then charges its tokens and tokens x
that cost to the budget. A call after the budget is spent raises
BudgetExhausted, which ends training normally. These checks run inside the
submission's process and catch honest mistakes; the grader re-checks the
model from outside (checks.py).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import copy
import importlib.util
import json
import math
import sys
import time
import traceback
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch import nn
from torch.utils.flop_counter import FlopCounterMode

from lmenv import task
from lmenv.api import Batch, BudgetExhausted, TrainContext


@dataclass
class Budget:
    tokens: float = task.TRAIN_TOKENS
    flops: float = task.TRAIN_FLOPS
    used_tokens: float = 0.0
    used_flops: float = 0.0
    started: float = field(default_factory=time.monotonic)

    def charge(self, tokens: float, flops: float) -> None:
        if self.exhausted:
            raise BudgetExhausted(self.describe())
        self.used_tokens += float(tokens)
        self.used_flops += float(flops)

    @property
    def elapsed_s(self) -> float:
        return time.monotonic() - self.started

    @property
    def exhausted(self) -> bool:
        return self.fraction_used >= 1.0

    @property
    def fraction_used(self) -> float:
        return max(self.used_tokens / self.tokens, self.used_flops / self.flops)

    def describe(self) -> str:
        return (f"{self.used_tokens:.3g}/{self.tokens:.3g} tokens, {self.used_flops:.3g}/{self.flops:.3g} FLOPs, "
                f"{self.elapsed_s:.0f} s")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- training and evaluation

def train_run(submission: Path, data: Path, out: Path, seed: int, device, tokens: float = task.TRAIN_TOKENS,
              log=print, corpus: int = task.CORPUS_TOKENS) -> dict:
    """Train one seed of the submission; writes OUT/model.pt and OUT/train.json and returns the record.

    ``tokens`` below TRAIN_TOKENS scales both budgets down (a quick proxy run).
    ``corpus`` (design experiments only) sets how many training tokens are sampled from.
    """

    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    vocab = json.loads((data / "meta.json").read_text())["vocab_size"]
    record: dict = {"seed": seed, "status": "error", "error": None, "budget_tokens": tokens, "corpus": corpus,
                    "history": []}

    def finish(**fields) -> dict:
        record.update(fields)
        (out / "train.json").write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2))
        return record

    try:
        torch.manual_seed(seed)
        np.random.seed(seed)
        model = build(submission, vocab, device)
        trainer = load_module(submission, "train")
        if trainer is None:
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"{submission}/train.py is missing")
    except Exception as error:
        return finish(error=f"building the model: {type(error).__name__}: {error}"[:2000])
    failures = interface_failures(model, vocab, device)
    if failures:
        return finish(error=failures[0])
    try:
        caps, failures = cap_failures(model, vocab, device)
    except Exception as error:
        return finish(error=f"measuring the model: {type(error).__name__}: {error}"[:2000])
    log(f"{caps['weights']['counted']:,} unique weights (+{caps['weights']['exempt']:,} embedding), "
        f"{caps['inference_flops_per_token'] / 1e6:.1f}M inference FLOPs per token")
    if failures:
        return finish(error="over the caps: " + "; ".join(failures), caps=caps)
    meter = Budget(tokens=tokens, flops=tokens * task.INFERENCE_FLOP_CAP)
    metered = MeteredModel(model, meter)
    stream = TokenStream(data, seed, corpus)
    stream.device = device
    history = record["history"]

    def record_log(entry: dict) -> None:
        entry = {**entry, "tokens": meter.used_tokens, "flops": meter.used_flops, "elapsed_s": round(meter.elapsed_s, 1)}
        history.append(entry)
        log("  ".join(f"{k} {v:.4g}" if isinstance(v, float) else f"{k} {v}" for k, v in entry.items()))

    ctx = TrainContext(model=metered, budget=meter, data=stream, config=read_config(submission), seed=seed,
                       device=device, log=record_log)
    status, error = "complete", None
    started = time.time()
    try:
        trainer.train(ctx)
    except BudgetExhausted:
        pass
    except Exception as e:  # the submission's code can fail in any way
        status, error = "error", f"train.py: {type(e).__name__}: {e}\n{traceback.format_exc()[-1500:]}"
    torch.save(saved_state(model), out / "model.pt")
    return finish(status=status, error=error, caps=caps, used_tokens=meter.used_tokens, used_flops=meter.used_flops,
                  call_costs=metered.cost_table(), train_seconds=time.time() - started,
                  corpus_passes=stream.passes + stream.next_block / stream.blocks)


_TOKEN_BYTES: dict = {}


def token_bytes(tokenizer: Path, eot: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """UTF-8 bytes of each token of our byte-level BPE (the end-of-text token counts as one byte)."""

    key = str(tokenizer)
    if key not in _TOKEN_BYTES:
        vocab = json.loads(tokenizer.read_text())["model"]["vocab"]
        # GPT-2's byte-to-character map: printable bytes stand for themselves, the rest from 256 up.
        printable = [*range(ord("!"), ord("~") + 1), *range(ord("¡"), ord("¬") + 1), *range(ord("®"), ord("ÿ") + 1)]
        to_char = {b: chr(b) for b in printable}
        extra = 0
        for b in range(256):
            if b not in to_char:
                to_char[b] = chr(256 + extra)
                extra += 1
        known = set(to_char.values())
        lengths = np.zeros(max(vocab.values()) + 1, dtype=np.int64)
        for piece, index in vocab.items():
            lengths[index] = len(piece) if all(c in known for c in piece) else len(piece.encode())
        lengths[eot] = 1
        _TOKEN_BYTES[key] = lengths
    return _TOKEN_BYTES[key]


@torch.no_grad()
def evaluate(model: nn.Module, tokens: np.ndarray, device, byte_lengths: np.ndarray | None = None,
             batch: int = 32) -> dict:
    """Mean next-token loss (nats per token) on every full SEQ_LEN window of ``tokens``, and bits per byte."""

    model.eval()
    n = (len(tokens) - 1) // task.SEQ_LEN
    total = torch.zeros((), dtype=torch.float64, device=device)
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    for start in range(0, n, batch):
        stop = min(n, start + batch)
        rows = np.stack([tokens[i * task.SEQ_LEN:(i + 1) * task.SEQ_LEN + 1] for i in range(start, stop)])
        rows = torch.from_numpy(rows.astype(np.int64)).to(device)
        with torch.autocast(device.type, dtype=torch.bfloat16, enabled=device.type == "cuda"):
            logits = model(rows[:, :-1])
        total += F.cross_entropy(logits.float().flatten(0, 1), rows[:, 1:].flatten(), reduction="sum")
    loss = total.item() / (n * task.SEQ_LEN)
    result = {"loss": loss if math.isfinite(loss) else float("inf"), "tokens": n * task.SEQ_LEN}
    if byte_lengths is not None:
        targets = np.asarray(tokens[1:n * task.SEQ_LEN + 1], dtype=np.int64)
        nbytes = int(byte_lengths[targets].sum())
        result["bits_per_byte"] = total.item() / math.log(2) / nbytes
        result["bytes"] = nbytes
    return result

"""Task constants shared by the agent's tools and the grader.

Changing a value here changes the task; the grader always uses its own clean
copy of this file.
"""

import os

# Unique weights: every parameter and buffer, tied tensors counted once,
# except one table of shape (vocab, width) with width <= EXEMPT_TABLE_WIDTH
# (the token embedding, or the output head tied to it).
WEIGHT_CAP = 3_000_000
EXEMPT_TABLE_WIDTH = 512
# FLOPs per token of one forward pass over a SEQ_LEN window (eval mode,
# attention included), measured with torch's FLOP counter. 120M (10x the
# starter) is about what one A100 trains on TRAIN_TOKENS within an hour.
INFERENCE_FLOP_CAP = 120e6
# Training: every token passed through the model counts (TRAIN_TOKENS in
# all, one pass over the CORPUS_TOKENS training text, each token seen once),
# and the forward FLOPs of all training calls together may not exceed
# INFERENCE_FLOP_CAP per token on average (TRAIN_FLOPS). Backward passes are
# not counted.
TRAIN_TOKENS = 250_000_000
CORPUS_TOKENS = 250_000_000
TRAIN_FLOPS = INFERENCE_FLOP_CAP * TRAIN_TOKENS
# Training reads only the first CORPUS_TOKENS tokens of train.bin, in windows
# of at most SEQ_LEN tokens; evaluation uses SEQ_LEN.
SEQ_LEN = 512
EVAL_TOKENS = 5_000_000
# Grading retrains a submission this many times on each test text (calibration uses 3).
SEEDS_PER_SUBMISSION = 1
# At most this many distinct ways of calling the model in training (each new
# one is measured once with the FLOP counter).
MAX_CALL_KINDS = 256

# Smoke tests of the pipeline only (tiny budget and data); never set in a real run.
if os.environ.get("LMENV_SMOKE"):
    TRAIN_TOKENS, CORPUS_TOKENS, EVAL_TOKENS = 1_000_000, 1_000_000, 100_000
    TRAIN_FLOPS = INFERENCE_FLOP_CAP * TRAIN_TOKENS
"""Command-line tools for the agent (the grader uses the same functions).

    python -m lmenv.run check --submission submission
    python -m lmenv.run train --submission submission --data data/lm --out runs/NAME --seed 0 [--tokens N]
    python -m lmenv.run eval --submission submission --run runs/NAME --data data/lm
    python -m lmenv.run score --submission submission --runs runs/A runs/B runs/C
    python -m lmenv.run snapshot --note "what changed and why" [--runs runs/A ...]

``check`` runs the rule checks the grader runs (caps, causality, static
scan). ``train`` trains one seed and evaluates it on the validation text.
A smaller ``--tokens`` scales the token and FLOP budgets down together for a
quick proxy run (not comparable with the starter's numbers). ``score``
averages several full-budget runs the way the grader does. ``snapshot``
saves a copy of submission/ with a note (not graded).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import shutil
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch

from lmenv import task
from lmenv.harness import evaluate, load_trained, token_bytes, train_run


def evaluate_run(submission: Path, run: Path, data: Path, device, log=print) -> dict:
    """Loss on DATA/val.bin (nats per token, and bits per byte); writes RUN/eval.json."""

    meta = json.loads((data / "meta.json").read_text())
    val = np.memmap(data / "val.bin", dtype=np.uint16, mode="r")[: task.EVAL_TOKENS]
    model = load_trained(submission, run, meta["vocab_size"], device)
    result = evaluate(model, val, device, token_bytes(data / "tokenizer.json", meta["eot"]))
    (run / "eval.json").write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
    log(f"validation loss {result['loss']:.4f} nats per token, {result['bits_per_byte']:.4f} bits per byte")
    return result


def summarize(evals: list[dict], reference: dict) -> dict:
    """Average per-seed evaluations and score them.

    reference: the starter's mean loss on the same text ("loss"), which scores 0.
    """

    loss = float(np.mean([e["loss"] for e in evals]))
    passed = bool(np.isfinite(loss))
    return {
        "seeds": len(evals),
        "loss": loss,
        "loss_by_seed": [e["loss"] for e in evals],
        "bits_per_byte": float(np.mean([e["bits_per_byte"] for e in evals])),
        "passed": passed,
        # Lower loss than the starter's, in nats; a failure scores as the starter does.
        "score": reference["loss"] - loss if passed else 0.0,
        "gain_vs_starter": reference["loss"] - loss if passed else 0.0,
    }


def snapshot(submission: Path, note: str, runs: list[str] | None) -> Path:
    """Copy the submission's code into snapshots/ next to it, with a note and a timestamp."""

    root = submission.resolve().parent / "snapshots"
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    index = 1 + max((int(p.name) for p in root.iterdir() if p.name.isdigit()), default=0)
    target = root / f"{index:03d}"
    shutil.copytree(submission, target, ignore=shutil.ignore_patterns("__pycache__", "*.pt"))
    meta = {"index": index, "note": note, "created_unix": time.time()}
    try:
        meta["episode_hours"] = round((time.time() - int(Path("/tmp/timer_start").read_text())) / 3600, 2)
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        pass
    if runs:
        meta["runs"] = runs
    (target / "snapshot.json").write_text(json.dumps(meta, indent=2))
    return target


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="python -m lmenv.run")
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)
    p = sub.add_parser("check")
    p.add_argument("--submission", default="submission")
    p.add_argument("--data", default="data/lm")
    for name in ("train", "_train"):
        p = sub.add_parser(name)
        p.add_argument("--submission", default="submission")
        p.add_argument("--data", default="data/lm")
        p.add_argument("--out", required=True)
        p.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
        p.add_argument("--tokens", type=float, default=task.TRAIN_TOKENS,
                       help="training tokens (smaller = quick proxy run, not comparable with the starter)")
        p.add_argument("--corpus", type=float, default=task.CORPUS_TOKENS, help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
    p = sub.add_parser("eval")
    p.add_argument("--submission", default="submission")
    p.add_argument("--run", required=True)
    p.add_argument("--data", default="data/lm")
    p = sub.add_parser("score")
    p.add_argument("--submission", default="submission")
    p.add_argument("--runs", nargs="+", required=True)
    p.add_argument("--data", default="data/lm")
    p = sub.add_parser("snapshot")
    p.add_argument("--submission", default="submission")
    p.add_argument("--note", required=True, help="what changed and why")
    p.add_argument("--runs", nargs="*", help="run folders that tested this version")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

    if args.cmd == "snapshot":
        print(f"saved snapshot {snapshot(Path(args.submission), args.note, args.runs)}")
        return 0
    device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    submission, data = Path(args.submission), Path(args.data)
    if args.cmd == "check":
        from lmenv.checks import run_checks

        vocab = json.loads((data / "meta.json").read_text())["vocab_size"]
        val = np.memmap(data / "val.bin", dtype=np.uint16, mode="r")[: task.SEQ_LEN]
        text = torch.from_numpy(val.astype(np.int64))[None].to(device)
        report = run_checks(submission, vocab, device, text=text)
        print(json.dumps(report.to_dict(), indent=2))
        print("PASSED" if report.passed else "FAILED:\n  " + "\n  ".join(report.failures))
        return 0 if report.passed else 1
    if args.cmd in ("train", "_train"):
        run = Path(args.out)
        record = train_run(submission, data, run, args.seed, device, tokens=args.tokens, corpus=int(args.corpus))
        if record["status"] == "error":
            print(f"training failed: {record['error']}")
            return 1
        if args.cmd == "train":
            evaluate_run(submission, run, data, device)
        return 0
    if args.cmd == "eval":
        evaluate_run(submission, Path(args.run), data, device)
        return 0
    if args.cmd == "score":
        evals = []
        for r in args.runs:
            path = Path(r) / "eval.json"
            if not path.exists():
                evaluate_run(submission, Path(r), data, device)
            evals.append(json.loads(path.read_text()))
        reference = json.loads((data / "reference.json").read_text())
        print(json.dumps(summarize(evals, reference), indent=2))
        return 0
    return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
"""Checks the grader runs on a submission, outside the training process.

* weights      unique floating-point weights against the cap, one embedding
               table exempt (harness.unique_weights)
* flops        inference FLOPs per token of model(ids) in eval mode, on random
               and on real text, against the cap
* interface    the model has an embed table, and with the harness's output
               layer (harness.OutputHead) is causal and gives [B, L, vocab] logits
               (harness.interface_failures)
* randomness   evaluation draws no random numbers (no weights generated from
               a seed inside forward) and two passes give identical logits
* call costs   every kind of training call recorded in train.json costs the
               same per token at batch size 1 and 2 and under two seeds, so
               the harness's once-per-kind measurement is honest
* state        persistent buffers and frozen parameters, listed for review
* static scan  source patterns that break the rules (network, subprocess,
               custom kernels, writing to the harness's counters, calling the
               unwrapped model in training) or that need a closer look
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import ast
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

import torch
from torch.utils._python_dispatch import TorchDispatchMode

from lmenv import task
from lmenv.harness import cap_failures, flops_per_token, interface_failures

RANDOM_OPS = {"rand", "randn", "randint", "randperm", "normal", "normal_", "uniform_", "bernoulli", "bernoulli_",
              "multinomial", "exponential_", "random_", "native_dropout", "rand_like", "randn_like", "randint_like"}


class RandomOps(TorchDispatchMode):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.seen: set[str] = set()

    def __torch_dispatch__(self, func, types, args=(), kwargs=None):
        name = func.overloadpacket.__name__
        if name in RANDOM_OPS:
            self.seen.add(name)
        return func(*args, **(kwargs or {}))


@dataclass
class CheckReport:
    weights: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    inference_flops_per_token: float = 0.0
    inference_flops_on_text: float = 0.0
    random_ops_in_eval: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    deterministic: bool = True
    call_costs: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    static_findings: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    state_notes: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    failures: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    @property
    def passed(self) -> bool:
        return not self.failures

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {**asdict(self), "passed": self.passed}


@torch.no_grad()
def check_eval(model, ids: torch.Tensor, text: torch.Tensor | None, report: CheckReport) -> None:
    """FLOPs on real text, random draws and determinism of model(ids) in eval mode."""

    if text is not None:
        report.inference_flops_on_text = flops_per_token(model, text)
        if report.inference_flops_on_text > task.INFERENCE_FLOP_CAP:
            report.failures.append(f"inference costs {report.inference_flops_on_text / 1e6:.1f}M FLOPs per token on "
                                   f"real text (cap {task.INFERENCE_FLOP_CAP / 1e6:.0f}M)")
    model.eval()
    mode = RandomOps()
    with mode:

        print(f"saved snapshot {snapshot(Path(args.submission), args.note, args.runs)}")
        return 0
    device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    submission, data = Path(args.submission), Path(args.data)
    if args.cmd == "check":
        from lmenv.checks import run_checks

        vocab = json.loads((data / "meta.json").read_text())["vocab_size"]
        val = np.memmap(data / "val.bin", dtype=np.uint16, mode="r")[: task.SEQ_LEN]
        text = torch.from_numpy(val.astype(np.int64))[None].to(device)
        report = run_checks(submission, vocab, device, text=text)
        print(json.dumps(report.to_dict(), indent=2))
        print("PASSED" if report.passed else "FAILED:\n  " + "\n  ".join(report.failures))
        return 0 if report.passed else 1
    if args.cmd in ("train", "_train"):
        run = Path(args.out)
        record = train_run(submission, data, run, args.seed, device, tokens=args.tokens, corpus=int(args.corpus))
        if record["status"] == "error":
            print(f"training failed: {record['error']}")
            return 1
        if args.cmd == "train":
            evaluate_run(submission, run, data, device)
        return 0
    if args.cmd == "eval":
        evaluate_run(submission, Path(args.run), data, device)
        return 0
    if args.cmd == "score":
        evals = []
        for r in args.runs:
            path = Path(r) / "eval.json"
            if not path.exists():
                evaluate_run(submission, Path(r), data, device)
            evals.append(json.loads(path.read_text()))
        reference = json.loads((data / "reference.json").read_text())
        print(json.dumps(summarize(evals, reference), indent=2))
        return 0
    return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
"""Checks the grader runs on a submission, outside the training process.

* weights      unique floating-point weights against the cap, one embedding
               table exempt (harness.unique_weights)
* flops        inference FLOPs per token of model(ids) in eval mode, on random
               and on real text, against the cap
* interface    the model has an embed table, and with the harness's output
               layer (harness.OutputHead) is causal and gives [B, L, vocab] logits
               (harness.interface_failures)
* randomness   evaluation draws no random numbers (no weights generated from
               a seed inside forward) and two passes give identical logits
* call costs   every kind of training call recorded in train.json costs the
               same per token at batch size 1 and 2 and under two seeds, so
               the harness's once-per-kind measurement is honest
* state        persistent buffers and frozen parameters, listed for review
* static scan  source patterns that break the rules (network, subprocess,
               custom kernels, writing to the harness's counters, calling the
               unwrapped model in training) or that need a closer look
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import ast
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

import torch
from torch.utils._python_dispatch import TorchDispatchMode

from lmenv import task
from lmenv.harness import cap_failures, flops_per_token, interface_failures

RANDOM_OPS = {"rand", "randn", "randint", "randperm", "normal", "normal_", "uniform_", "bernoulli", "bernoulli_",
              "multinomial", "exponential_", "random_", "native_dropout", "rand_like", "randn_like", "randint_like"}


class RandomOps(TorchDispatchMode):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.seen: set[str] = set()

    def __torch_dispatch__(self, func, types, args=(), kwargs=None):
        name = func.overloadpacket.__name__
        if name in RANDOM_OPS:
            self.seen.add(name)
        return func(*args, **(kwargs or {}))


@dataclass
class CheckReport:
    weights: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    inference_flops_per_token: float = 0.0
    inference_flops_on_text: float = 0.0
    random_ops_in_eval: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    deterministic: bool = True
    call_costs: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    static_findings: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    state_notes: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    failures: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    @property
    def passed(self) -> bool:
        return not self.failures

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {**asdict(self), "passed": self.passed}


@torch.no_grad()
def check_eval(model, ids: torch.Tensor, text: torch.Tensor | None, report: CheckReport) -> None:
    """FLOPs on real text, random draws and determinism of model(ids) in eval mode."""

    if text is not None:
        report.inference_flops_on_text = flops_per_token(model, text)
        if report.inference_flops_on_text > task.INFERENCE_FLOP_CAP:
            report.failures.append(f"inference costs {report.inference_flops_on_text / 1e6:.1f}M FLOPs per token on "
                                   f"real text (cap {task.INFERENCE_FLOP_CAP / 1e6:.0f}M)")
    model.eval()
    mode = RandomOps()
    with mode:

with mode:
        first = model(ids)
    report.random_ops_in_eval = sorted(mode.seen)
    if mode.seen:
        report.failures.append("evaluation draws random numbers (" + ", ".join(sorted(mode.seen)) + "); "
                               "model(ids) in eval mode must be deterministic")
    report.deterministic = torch.equal(first, model(ids))
    if not report.deterministic:
        report.failures.append("two evaluation passes give different logits")


def check_call_costs(model, recorded: list[dict], vocab_size: int, device, report: CheckReport) -> None:
    """Each recorded kind of training call costs the same per token at batch 1 and 2 and under two seeds."""

    for call in recorded:
        gen = torch.Generator().manual_seed(0)
        ids = torch.randint(0, vocab_size, (2, call["length"]), generator=gen).to(device)
        costs = []
        for rows, seed in ((1, 0), (2, 0), (1, 1)):
            torch.manual_seed(seed)
            costs.append(flops_per_token(model, ids[:rows], call["kwargs"], training=call["training"], grad=True))
        entry = {**call, "measured": costs}
        report.call_costs.append(entry)
        if max(costs) > 1.01 * min(costs) + 1:
            report.failures.append(f"the cost of model(ids, **{call['kwargs']}) (training={call['training']}) "
                                   f"depends on the batch size or on random draws: {[round(c) for c in costs]} "
                                   "FLOPs per token")


def check_state(model, report: CheckReport) -> None:
    """List state that is not trained by gradient descent; a reviewer decides if it is legitimate."""

    parameter_names = {name for name, _ in model.named_parameters()}
    for name, value in model.state_dict().items():
        if name not in parameter_names:
            report.state_notes.append(f"persistent buffer {name} {tuple(value.shape)} {value.dtype}")
    for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
        if not parameter.requires_grad:
            report.state_notes.append(f"frozen parameter {name} {tuple(parameter.shape)}")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- static scan

BANNED_MODULES = {"socket", "urllib", "http", "requests", "httpx", "aiohttp", "ftplib", "smtplib",
                  "subprocess", "ctypes", "pty",
                  # Kernels launched outside torch's dispatcher are invisible to the FLOP counter.
                  "triton", "numba", "cupy", "jax", "torch.utils.cpp_extension", "torch.library"}
REVIEW_MODULES = {"importlib", "inspect", "gc", "pickle", "shutil", "glob"}
ALLOWED_HARNESS = {"lmenv.api", "lmenv.task"}
PROTECTED_ATTRIBUTES = {"budget", "used_tokens", "used_flops", "costs", "inner", "charge",
                        "exhausted"}
REVIEW_BUILTINS = {"open", "exec", "eval", "__import__", "setattr"}
REVIEW_METHODS = {"load", "loadtxt", "fromfile", "system", "popen", "_getframe", "currentframe",
                  "get_objects", "getenv", "manual_seed"}


def _finding(severity: str, rule: str, path: Path, node: ast.AST, source: list[str]) -> dict:
    line = getattr(node, "lineno", 0)
    return {"severity": severity, "rule": rule, "file": path.name, "line": line,
            "code": source[line - 1].strip() if 0 < line <= len(source) else ""}


def _unwrapped(node: ast.AST) -> bool:
    """ctx.model.inner or ctx.model.inner.body: the model without the meter."""

    return isinstance(node, ast.Attribute) and (
        node.attr == "inner" or (node.attr == "body" and isinstance(node.value, ast.Attribute)
                                 and node.value.attr == "inner"))


def static_scan(directory: Path) -> list[dict]:
    findings = []
    for path in sorted(Path(directory).rglob("*.py")):
        text = path.read_text()
        source = text.splitlines()
        try:
            tree = ast.parse(text)
        except SyntaxError as error:
            findings.append({"severity": "violation", "rule": f"syntax error: {error}", "file": path.name,
                             "line": error.lineno or 0, "code": ""})
            continue
        inner_names = {
            target.id
            for node in ast.walk(tree) if isinstance(node, ast.Assign)
            and _unwrapped(node.value)
            for target in node.targets if isinstance(target, ast.Name)
        }
        for node in ast.walk(tree):
            if isinstance(node, (ast.Import, ast.ImportFrom)):
                if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
                    names = [a.name for a in node.names]
                else:  # "from torch.utils import cpp_extension" imports torch.utils.cpp_extension
                    names = [node.module or ""] + [f"{node.module}.{a.name}" for a in node.names if node.module]
                for name in names:
                    root = name.split(".")[0]
                    if any(name == b or name.startswith(b + ".") for b in BANNED_MODULES):
                        findings.append(_finding("violation", f"imports {name}", path, node, source))
                    elif root in REVIEW_MODULES:
                        findings.append(_finding("review", f"imports {name}", path, node, source))
                    elif root == "lmenv" and not any(name == a or name.startswith(a + ".") for a in ALLOWED_HARNESS):
                        findings.append(_finding("violation", f"imports harness internals ({name})", path, node, source))
            elif isinstance(node, (ast.Assign, ast.AugAssign, ast.AnnAssign)):
                targets = node.targets if isinstance(node, ast.Assign) else [node.target]
                for target in targets:
                    if isinstance(target, ast.Attribute) and target.attr in PROTECTED_ATTRIBUTES:
                        findings.append(_finding("violation", f"writes harness attribute .{target.attr}", path, node, source))
            elif isinstance(node, ast.Call):
                func = node.func
                target = func.value if isinstance(func, ast.Attribute) and func.attr in ("forward", "__call__") else func
                if _unwrapped(target) or (
                    isinstance(target, ast.Name) and target.id in inner_names
                ):
                    findings.append(_finding("violation", "calls the unwrapped model (not charged to the budget)",
                                             path, node, source))
                if (isinstance(func, ast.Name) and func.id in REVIEW_BUILTINS) or (
                    isinstance(func, ast.Attribute) and func.attr in REVIEW_METHODS
                ):
                    name = func.id if isinstance(func, ast.Name) else func.attr
                    findings.append(_finding("review", f"calls {name}()", path, node, source))
            elif isinstance(node, ast.Attribute) and node.attr == "modules" and getattr(node.value, "id", "") == "sys":
                findings.append(_finding("violation", "touches sys.modules", path, node, source))
    return findings


def run_static_scan(directory: Path, report: CheckReport) -> None:
    report.static_findings = static_scan(directory)
    violations = [f for f in report.static_findings if f["severity"] == "violation"]
    if violations:
        report.failures.append(f"{len(violations)} static-scan violation(s): " +
                               "; ".join(f"{v['file']}:{v['line']} {v['rule']}" for v in violations[:5]))


def run_checks(submission: Path, vocab_size: int, device, model=None, text: torch.Tensor | None = None,
               recorded_calls: list[dict] | None = None) -> CheckReport:
    """All checks on a submission.

    ``model`` is the trained model if there is one (else a fresh build),
    ``text`` one SEQ_LEN window of real token ids, ``recorded_calls`` the
    call_costs of a training run (train.json).
    """

    from lmenv.harness import build

    report = CheckReport()
    run_static_scan(submission, report)
    try:
        if model is None:
            torch.manual_seed(0)
            model = build(submission, vocab_size, device)
    except Exception as error:  # the submission's code can fail in any way
        report.failures.append(f"building the model: {type(error).__name__}: {error}"[:500])
        return report
    check_state(model, report)
    report.failures.extend(interface_failures(model, vocab_size, device, perturb=False))
    ids = torch.randint(0, vocab_size, (2, 128), generator=torch.Generator().manual_seed(0)).to(device)
    try:
        caps, failures = cap_failures(model, vocab_size, device)
        report.weights, report.inference_flops_per_token = caps["weights"], caps["inference_flops_per_token"]
        report.failures.extend(failures)
        check_eval(model, ids, text, report)
        check_call_costs(model, recorded_calls or [], vocab_size, device, report)
    except Exception as error:
        report.failures.append(f"measuring the model: {type(error).__name__}: {error}"[:500])
    return report

#7 Write/workspace/submission/model.py
"""Looped (weight-shared) transformer: few unique weights, many FLOPs per token.

The token embedding (8192 x d_model, d_model <= 512, exempt from the weight
cap) feeds a residual stream of width d_model. A small set of unique blocks
(n_blocks) is applied n_loops times in sequence (depth n_blocks * n_loops).
Each application gets its own cheap per-loop parameters (RMSNorm gains,
residual-branch scales and an input-injection scale) so the shared blocks can
behave differently at each depth. Attention uses RoPE and QK-RMSNorm; the MLP
uses squared ReLU. The model returns RMS-normed hidden states; the harness's
tied output head turns them into logits.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass

import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch import nn


@dataclass
class ModelConfig:
    vocab_size: int = 8192
    d_model: int = 512
    n_heads: int = 8
    n_blocks: int = 1
    n_loops: int = 16
    mlp_width: int = 1792
    rope_theta: float = 10_000.0
    act: str = "relu2"
    qk_norm: bool = True
    inject: bool = True
    embed_std: float = 0.02
    out_init: float = 0.0  # std multiplier for output projections (0 = zero init)


def _rotate_half(x):
    first, second = x.chunk(2, dim=-1)
    return torch.cat((-second, first), dim=-1)


class Block(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        d = cfg.d_model
        self.n_heads = cfg.n_heads
        self.head_dim = d // cfg.n_heads
        self.qk_norm = cfg.qk_norm
        self.act = cfg.act
        self.q = nn.Linear(d, d, bias=False)
        self.k = nn.Linear(d, d, bias=False)
        self.v = nn.Linear(d, d, bias=False)
        self.o = nn.Linear(d, d, bias=False)
        self.fc = nn.Linear(d, cfg.mlp_width, bias=False)
        self.proj = nn.Linear(cfg.mlp_width, d, bias=False)

    def forward(self, x, cos, sin, g1, g2, sa, sm):
        b, length, d = x.shape
        h = F.rms_norm(x, (d,)) * g1
        q = self.q(h).view(b, length, self.n_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)
        k = self.k(h).view(b, length, self.n_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)
        v = self.v(h).view(b, length, self.n_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)
        if self.qk_norm:
            q = F.rms_norm(q, (self.head_dim,))
            k = F.rms_norm(k, (self.head_dim,))
        q = q * cos + _rotate_half(q) * sin
        k = k * cos + _rotate_half(k) * sin
        y = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
        x = x + sa * self.o(y.transpose(1, 2).reshape(b, length, d))
        h = F.rms_norm(x, (d,)) * g2
        u = self.fc(h)
        if self.act == "relu2":
            u = F.relu(u).square()
        else:
            u = F.gelu(u, approximate="tanh")
        return x + sm * self.proj(u)


class LoopedLM(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.cfg = cfg
        d = cfg.d_model
        depth = cfg.n_blocks * cfg.n_loops
        self.embed = nn.Embedding(cfg.vocab_size, d)
        self.blocks = nn.ModuleList(Block(cfg) for _ in range(cfg.n_blocks))
        # per-application parameters (cheap: a few vectors per layer application)
        self.g1 = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(depth, d))
        self.g2 = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(depth, d))
        self.sa = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(depth, 1))
        self.sm = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(depth, 1))
        self.inj = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(cfg.n_loops, 1))
        self.g_out = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(d))
        head_dim = d // cfg.n_heads
        inv_freq = 1.0 / cfg.rope_theta ** (torch.arange(0, head_dim, 2, dtype=torch.float32) / head_dim)
        self.register_buffer("inv_freq", inv_freq, persistent=False)

        nn.init.normal_(self.embed.weight, std=cfg.embed_std)
        for block in self.blocks:
            for lin in (block.q, block.k, block.v, block.fc):
                nn.init.normal_(lin.weight, std=1.0 / math.sqrt(lin.in_features))
            for lin in (block.o, block.proj):
                if cfg.out_init > 0:
                    nn.init.normal_(lin.weight, std=cfg.out_init / math.sqrt(lin.in_features * depth))
                else:
                    nn.init.zeros_(lin.weight)
        self.block_fn = self._block_call  # train.py may replace with a compiled version

    @staticmethod
    def _block_call(block, x, cos, sin, g1, g2, sa, sm):
        return block(x, cos, sin, g1, g2, sa, sm)

    def forward(self, input_ids):
        cfg = self.cfg
        length = input_ids.shape[1]
        positions = torch.arange(length, device=input_ids.device, dtype=torch.float32)
        angles = torch.outer(positions, self.inv_freq)
        angles = torch.cat((angles, angles), dim=-1)[None, None]
        cos, sin = angles.cos(), angles.sin()
        e = F.rms_norm(self.embed(input_ids), (cfg.d_model,))
        x = e
        i = 0
        for loop in range(cfg.n_loops):
            if cfg.inject and loop > 0:
                x = x + self.inj[loop] * e
            for block in self.blocks:
                x = self.block_fn(block, x, cos, sin, self.g1[i], self.g2[i], self.sa[i], self.sm[i])
                i += 1
        return F.rms_norm(x, (cfg.d_model,)) * self.g_out


def build_model(config: dict) -> LoopedLM:
    """config is config.json plus {"vocab_size": ...} from the harness."""

    return LoopedLM(ModelConfig(vocab_size=config["vocab_size"], **config.get("model", {})))

Attention(nn.Module):\n    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.n_heads = cfg.n_heads\n        self.head_dim = cfg.d_model // cfg.n_heads\n        self.c_attn = nn.Linear(cfg.d_model, 3 * cfg.d_model)\n        self.c_proj = nn.Linear(cfg.d_model, cfg.d_model)\n\n    def forward(self, x, cos, sin):\n        batch, length, width = x.shape\n        q, k, v = self.c_attn(x).split(width, dim=2)\n        q, k, v = (t.view(batch, length, self.n_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2) for t in (q, k, v))\n        q, k = q * cos + _rotate_half(q) * sin, k * cos + _rotate_half(k) * sin\n        out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)\n        return self.c_proj(out.transpose(1, 2).reshape(batch, length, width))\n\n\nclass super().__init__()\n        self.ln_1 = nn.LayerNorm(cfg.d_model, eps=cfg.layer_norm_eps)\n        self.attn = Attention(cfg)\n        self.ln_2 = nn.LayerNorm(cfg.d_model, eps=cfg.layer_norm_eps)\n        self.mlp = MLP(cfg)\n\n    def forward(self, x, cos, sin):\n        x = x + self.attn(self.ln_1(x), cos, sin)\n        return x + self.mlp(self.ln_2(x))\n\n\ndef _rotate_half(x):\n    first, second = x.chunk(2, dim=-1)\n    return torch.cat((-second, first), dim=-1)\n\n\nclass GPT(nn.Module):\n    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.cfg = cfg\n        self.embed = nn.Embedding(cfg.vocab_size, cfg.d_model)\n        self.blocks = nn.ModuleList(Block(cfg) for _ in range(cfg.n_layers))\n        self.ln_f = nn.LayerNorm(cfg.d_model, eps=cfg.layer_norm_eps)\n        head_dim = cfg.d_model // cfg.n_heads\n        inv_freq = 1.0 / cfg.rope_theta ** (torch.arange(0, head_dim, 2, dtype=torch.float32) / head_dim)\n        self.register_buffer(\"inv_freq\", inv_freq, persistent=False)\n        nn.init.normal_(self.embed.weight, std=0.02)\n        for module in self.blocks.modules():\n            if isinstance(module, nn.Linear):\n                nn.init.normal_(module.weight, std=0.02)\n                nn.init.zeros_(module.bias)\n        for block in self.blocks:\n            nn.init.normal_(block.attn.c_proj.weight, std=0.02 / math.sqrt(2 * cfg.n_layers))\n            nn.init.normal_(block.mlp.c_proj.weight, std=0.02 / math.sqrt(2 * cfg.n_layers))\n        self.stack = self.apply_blocks  # train.py replaces this with a compiled version\n\n    def apply_blocks(self, h, cos, sin):\n        for block in self.blocks:\n            h = block(h, cos, sin)\n        return h\n\n    def forward(self, input_ids):\n        length = input_ids.shape[1]\n        positions = torch.arange(length, device=input_ids.device, dtype=torch.float32)\n        angles = torch.outer(positions, self.inv_freq)\n        angles = torch.cat((angles, angles), dim=-1)[None, None]\n        h = self.stack(self.embed(input_ids), angles.cos(), angles.sin())\n        return self.ln_f(h)  # the harness applies the tied output head\n\n\ndef build_model(config: dict) -> GPT:\n    \"\"\"config is config.json plus {\"vocab_size\": ...} from the harness.\"\"\"\n\n    return GPT(ModelConfig(vocab_size=config[\"vocab_size\"], **config.get(\"model\", {})))\n", "userModified": false}

The FLOP counter's module tracker chokes on parameter slices passed into a submodule call under no_grad. I'll apply blocks via a plain function using F.linear instead of a module __call__.

Ran 1 commandBash ×1
#10 Bashpython - <<'EOF'
python - <<'EOF'
import re
p = "submission/model.py"
s = open(p).read()
old_start = s.index("    def forward(self, x, cos, sin, g1, g2, sa, sm):")
old_end = s.index("class LoopedLM")
new = '''

def apply_block(blk: Block, x, cos, sin, g1, g2, sa, sm):
    """One application of a (shared) block; a plain function so per-loop tensors can be passed in."""

    b, length, d = x.shape
    nh, hd = blk.n_heads, blk.head_dim
    h = F.rms_norm(x, (d,)) * g1
    q = F.linear(h, blk.q.weight).view(b, length, nh, hd).transpose(1, 2)
    k = F.linear(h, blk.k.weight).view(b, length, nh, hd).transpose(1, 2)
    v = F.linear(h, blk.v.weight).view(b, length, nh, hd).transpose(1, 2)
    if blk.qk_norm:
        q = F.rms_norm(q, (hd,))
        k = F.rms_norm(k, (hd,))
    q = q * cos + _rotate_half(q) * sin
    k = k * cos + _rotate_half(k) * sin
    y = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
    x = x + sa * F.linear(y.transpose(1, 2).reshape(b, length, d), blk.o.weight)
    h = F.rms_norm(x, (d,)) * g2
    u = F.linear(h, blk.fc.weight)
    if blk.act == "relu2":
        u = F.relu(u).square()
    else:
        u = F.gelu(u, approximate="tanh")
    return x + sm * F.linear(u, blk.proj.weight)


'''
s = s[:old_start].rstrip() + "\n" + new + s[old_end:]
s = s.replace('''        self.block_fn = self._block_call  # train.py may replace with a compiled version

    @staticmethod
    def _block_call(block, x, cos, sin, g1, g2, sa, sm):
        return block(x, cos, sin, g1, g2, sa, sm)
''', '''        self.block_fn = apply_block  # train.py may replace with a compiled version
''')
open(p, "w").write(s)
