Now the training script with Muon (for matrices) + Adam (embedding/scalars) and a warmup-stable-decay schedule:

2 tool callsWrite ×1, Bash ×1
#11 Write/workspace/submission/train.py
"""Training recipe: next-token loss, Muon for hidden matrices, Adam for the rest.

Random 512-token windows, bf16 autocast. Hidden weight matrices are trained
with Muon (momentum SGD whose update is orthogonalised by a Newton-Schulz
iteration); the tied token embedding and the per-loop gains/scales use Adam.
Learning rate: short linear warmup, constant, then linear decay to a small
fraction over the last part of the budget. Training stops when the budget is
spent.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math

import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F


def zeropower_via_newtonschulz5(G: torch.Tensor, steps: int = 5) -> torch.Tensor:
    a, b, c = (3.4445, -4.7750, 2.0315)
    X = G.bfloat16()
    transposed = G.size(-2) > G.size(-1)
    if transposed:
        X = X.mT
    X = X / (X.norm(dim=(-2, -1), keepdim=True) + 1e-7)
    for _ in range(steps):
        A = X @ X.mT
        B = b * A + c * A @ A
        X = a * X + B @ X
    if transposed:
        X = X.mT
    return X


class Muon(torch.optim.Optimizer):
    def __init__(self, params, lr=0.02, momentum=0.95, nesterov=True, ns_steps=5, weight_decay=0.0):
        super().__init__(params, dict(lr=lr, momentum=momentum, nesterov=nesterov, ns_steps=ns_steps,
                                      weight_decay=weight_decay))

    @torch.no_grad()
    def step(self):
        for group in self.param_groups:
            for p in group["params"]:
                if p.grad is None:
                    continue
                g = p.grad
                state = self.state[p]
                if "buf" not in state:
                    state["buf"] = torch.zeros_like(g)
                buf = state["buf"]
                buf.lerp_(g, 1 - group["momentum"])
                g = g.lerp(buf, group["momentum"]) if group["nesterov"] else buf
                u = zeropower_via_newtonschulz5(g, group["ns_steps"]).to(p.dtype)
                scale = max(1.0, p.size(-2) / p.size(-1)) ** 0.5
                if group["weight_decay"]:
                    p.mul_(1 - group["lr"] * group["weight_decay"])
                p.add_(u, alpha=-group["lr"] * scale)


def train(ctx):
    cfg = ctx.config["train"]
    model, budget = ctx.model, ctx.budget
    body = model.inner.body
    if cfg.get("compile", True) and ctx.device.type == "cuda":
        body.block_fn = torch.compile(body.block_fn)

    named = list(body.named_parameters())
    matrices = [p for n, p in named if p.dim() == 2 and n.startswith("blocks.")]
    embed = [body.embed.weight]
    others = [p for n, p in named if not (p.dim() == 2 and n.startswith("blocks.")) and p is not body.embed.weight]
    muon = Muon(matrices, lr=cfg["muon_lr"], momentum=cfg.get("muon_momentum", 0.95),
                weight_decay=cfg.get("muon_wd", 0.0))
    adam = torch.optim.AdamW([
        {"params": embed, "lr": cfg["embed_lr"], "weight_decay": cfg.get("embed_wd", 0.0)},
        {"params": others, "lr": cfg["scalar_lr"], "weight_decay": 0.0},
    ], betas=(cfg.get("beta1", 0.9), cfg.get("beta2", 0.95)), eps=1e-10, fused=ctx.device.type == "cuda")
    for opt in (muon, adam):
        for group in opt.param_groups:
            group["base_lr"] = group["lr"]

    warmup, decay, final = cfg.get("warmup", 0.01), cfg.get("decay", 0.4), cfg.get("final_lr", 0.0)

    def lr_scale(progress: float) -> float:
        if progress < warmup:
            return max(progress, 1e-3) / warmup
        if progress < 1 - decay:
            return 1.0
        p = (progress - (1 - decay)) / decay
        return final + (1 - final) * max(0.0, 1 - p)

    params = [p for _, p in named]
    update = 0
    model.train()
    accumulation, micro = cfg["accumulation"], cfg["micro_batch"]
    clip = cfg.get("clip", 1.0)
    while not budget.exhausted:
        s = lr_scale(budget.fraction_used)
        for opt in (muon, adam):
            for group in opt.param_groups:
                group["lr"] = group["base_lr"] * s
        # Muon momentum warmup
        if cfg.get("momentum_warmup", 0):
            frac = min(1.0, update / cfg["momentum_warmup"])
            for group in muon.param_groups:
                group["momentum"] = (1 - frac) * 0.85 + frac * cfg.get("muon_momentum", 0.95)
        total = 0.0
        for _ in range(accumulation):
            batch = ctx.data.sample(micro)
            with torch.autocast(ctx.device.type, dtype=torch.bfloat16):
                logits = model(batch.tokens)
            loss = F.cross_entropy(logits.float().flatten(0, 1), batch.targets.flatten())
            (loss / accumulation).backward()
            total += loss.detach() / accumulation
        if clip:
            torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params, clip)
        muon.step()
        adam.step()
        muon.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        adam.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        update += 1
        if update % cfg["log_every"] == 0:
            total = total.item()
            if not math.isfinite(total):
                raise FloatingPointError(f"loss is {total} at update {update}")
            ctx.log({"update": update, "loss": total, "lr_scale": s})

decay:\n            return 1.0\n        p = (progress - (1 - decay)) / decay\n        return final + (1 - final) * max(0.0, 1 - p)\n\n    params = [p for _, p in named]\n    update = 0\n    model.train()\n    accumulation, micro = cfg[\"accumulation\"], cfg[\"micro_batch\"]\n    clip = cfg.get(\"clip\", 1.0)\n    while not budget.exhausted:\n        s = lr_scale(budget.fraction_used)\n        for opt in (muon, adam):\n            for group in opt.param_groups:\n                group[\"lr\"] = group[\"base_lr\"] * s\n        # Muon momentum warmup\n        if cfg.get(\"momentum_warmup\", 0):\n            frac = min(1.0, update / cfg[\"momentum_warmup\"])\n            for group in muon.param_groups:\n                group[\"momentum\"] = (1 - frac) * 0.85 + frac * cfg.get(\"muon_momentum\", 0.95)\n        total = 0.0\n        for _ in range(accumulation):\n            batch = ctx.data.sample(micro)\n            with torch.autocast(ctx.device.type, dtype=torch.bfloat16):\n                logits = model(batch.tokens)\n            loss = F.cross_entropy(logits.float().flatten(0, 1), batch.targets.flatten())\n            (loss / accumulation).backward()\n            total += loss.detach() / accumulation\n        if clip:\n            torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params, clip)\n        muon.step()\n        adam.step()\n        muon.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)\n        adam.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)\n        update += 1\n        if update % cfg[\"log_every\"] == 0:\n            total = total.item()\n            if not math.isfinite(total):\n                raise FloatingPointError(f\"loss is {total} at update {update}\")\n            ctx.log({\"update\": update, \"loss\": total, \"lr_scale\": s})\n", "structuredPatch": [{"oldStart": 1, "oldLines": 9, "newStart": 1, "newLines": 11, "lines": ["-\"\"\"Starting training recipe: plain next-token loss.", "+\"\"\"Training recipe: next-token loss, Muon for hidden matrices, Adam for the rest.", " ", "-Random 512-token windows, AdamW (betas 0.9, 0.95; weight decay on matrices", "-only), bf16 autocast, gradient clipping at 1, a linear warmup over the first", "-5% of the budget and a cosine decay to 10% of the learning rate over the", "-rest. #14 Bashcat > /workspace/tools/run_variant.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/tools/run_variant.py <<'EOF'
"""Make variants/NAME = submission/ + config overrides, then train it in the background.
usage: python tools/run_variant.py NAME '{"model":{...},"train":{...}}' [--tokens N] [--seed S] [--src DIR]
"""
import argparse, json, shutil, subprocess, os
from pathlib import Path
ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
ap.add_argument("name"); ap.add_argument("over", nargs="?", default="{}")
ap.add_argument("--tokens", type=float, default=250e6); ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
ap.add_argument("--src", default="submission")
a = ap.parse_args()
root = Path("/workspace")
dst = root / "variants" / a.name
if dst.exists(): shutil.rmtree(dst)
shutil.copytree(root / a.src, dst, ignore=shutil.ignore_patterns("__pycache__"))
cfg = json.loads((dst / "config.json").read_text())
over = json.loads(a.over)
for sec, vals in over.items():
    cfg.setdefault(sec, {}).update(vals)
(dst / "config.json").write_text(json.dumps(cfg, indent=2))
run = f"runs/{a.name}_s{a.seed}"
log = open(root / f"{run}.out", "w")
subprocess.Popen(["python", "-m", "lmenv.run", "train", "--submission", str(dst), "--out", run, "--seed", str(a.seed),
                  "--tokens", str(int(a.tokens))], cwd=root, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, start_new_session=True)
print("launched", run)
EOF
cat > /workspace/tools/results.sh <<'EOF'
#!/bin/bash
# print final val loss of runs matching pattern
cd /workspace/runs
for f in ${1:-*}.out; do
  v=$(grep -o "validation loss [0-9.]*" $f | awk '{print $3}')
  last=$(grep "^update" $f | tail -1 | awk '{print $4, "t="$12}')
  echo "$f  val=${v:-...}  last: $last"
done
EOF
chmod +x /workspace/tools/results.sh; sleep 30; tail -3 runs/v1_s0.out

#18 Read/workspace/submission/model.py
20	from torch import nn
21	
22	
23	@dataclass
24	class ModelConfig:
25	    vocab_size: int = 8192
26	    d_model: int = 512
27	    n_heads: int = 8
28	    n_blocks: int = 1
29	    n_loops: int = 16
30	    mlp_width: int = 1792
31	    rope_theta: float = 10_000.0
32	    act: str = "relu2"
33	    qk_norm: bool = True
34	    inject: bool = True
35	    embed_std: float = 0.02
36	    out_init: float = 0.0  # std multiplier for output projections (0 = zero init)
37	
38	
39	def _rotate_half(x):
40	    first, second = x.chunk(2, dim=-1)
41	    return torch.cat((-second, first), dim=-1)
42	
43	
44	class Block(nn.Module):
45	    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
46	        super().__init__()
47	        d = cfg.d_model
48	        self.n_heads = cfg.n_heads
49	        self.head_dim = d // cfg.n_heads
50	        self.qk_norm = cfg.qk_norm
51	        self.act = cfg.act
52	        self.q = nn.Linear(d, d, bias=False)
53	        self.k = nn.Linear(d, d, bias=False)
54	        self.v = nn.Linear(d, d, bias=False)
55	        self.o = nn.Linear(d, d, bias=False)
56	        self.fc = nn.Linear(d, cfg.mlp_width, bias=False)
57	        self.proj = nn.Linear(cfg.mlp_width, d, bias=False)
58	
59	
60	def apply_block(blk: Block, x, cos, sin, g1, g2, sa, sm):
61	    """One application of a (shared) block; a plain function so per-loop tensors can be passed in."""
62	
63	    b, length, d = x.shape
64	    nh, hd = blk.n_heads, blk.head_dim
65	    h = F.rms_norm(x, (d,)) * g1
66	    q = F.linear(h, blk.q.weight).view(b, length, nh, hd).transpose(1, 2)
67	    k = F.linear(h, blk.k.weight).view(b, length, nh, hd).transpose(1, 2)
68	    v = F.linear(h, blk.v.weight).view(b, length, nh, hd).transpose(1, 2)
69	    if blk.qk_norm:
70	        q = F.rms_norm(q, (hd,))
71	        k = F.rms_norm(k, (hd,))
72	    q = q * cos + _rotate_half(q) * sin
73	    k = k * cos + _rotate_half(k) * sin
74	    y = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
75	    x = x + sa * F.linear(y.transpose(1, 2).reshape(b, length, d), blk.o.weight)
76	    h = F.rms_norm(x, (d,)) * g2
77	    u = F.linear(h, blk.fc.weight)
78	    if blk.act == "relu2":
79	        u = F.relu(u).square()
80	    else:
81	        u = F.gelu(u, approximate="tanh")
82	    return x + sm * F.linear(u, blk.proj.weight)
83	
84	
85	class LoopedLM(nn.Module):
86	    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
87	        super().__init__()
88	        self.cfg = cfg
89	        d = cfg.d_model
90	        depth = cfg.n_blocks * cfg.n_loops
91	        self.embed = nn.Embedding(cfg.vocab_size, d)
92	        self.blocks = nn.ModuleList(Block(cfg) for _ in range(cfg.n_blocks))
93	        # per-application parameters (cheap: a few vectors per layer application)
94	        self.g1 = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(depth, d))
95	        self.g2 = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(depth, d))
96	        self.sa = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(depth, 1))
97	        self.sm = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(depth, 1))
98	        self.inj = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(cfg.n_loops, 1))
99	        self.g_out = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(d))
100	        head_dim = d // cfg.n_heads
101	        inv_freq = 1.0 / cfg.rope_theta ** (torch.arange(0, head_dim, 2, dtype=torch.float32) / head_dim)
102	        self.register_buffer("inv_freq", inv_freq, persistent=False)
103	
104	        nn.init.normal_(self.embed.weight, std=cfg.embed_std)
105	        for block in self.blocks:
106	            for lin in (block.q, block.k, block.v, block.fc):
107	                nn.init.normal_(lin.weight, std=1.0 / math.sqrt(lin.in_features))
108	            for lin in (block.o, block.proj):
109	                if cfg.out_init > 0:
110	                    nn.init.normal_(lin.weight, std=cfg.out_init / math.sqrt(lin.in_features * depth))
111	                else:
112	                    nn.init.zeros_(lin.weight)
113	        self.block_fn = apply_block  # train.py may replace with a compiled version
114	
115	    def forward(self, input_ids):
116	        cfg = self.cfg
117	        length = input_ids.shape[1]
118	        positions = torch.arange(length, device=input_ids.device, dtype=torch.float32)
119	        angles = torch.outer(positions, self.inv_freq)
120	        angles = torch.cat((angles, angles), dim=-1)[None, None]
121	        cos, sin = angles.cos(), angles.sin()
122	        e = F.rms_norm(self.embed(input_ids), (cfg.d_model,))
123	        x = e
124	        i = 0
125	        for loop in range(cfg.n_loops):
126	            if cfg.inject and loop > 0:
127	                x = x + self.inj[loop] * e
128	            for block in self.blocks:
129	                x = self.block_fn(block, x, cos, sin, self.g1[i], self.g2[i], self.sa[i], self.sm[i])
130	                i += 1
131	        return F.rms_norm(x, (cfg.d_model,)) * self.g_out
132	
133	
134	def build_model(config: dict) -> LoopedLM:
135	    """config is config.json plus {"vocab_size": ...} from the harness."""
136	
137	    return LoopedLM(ModelConfig(vocab_size=config["vocab_size"], **config.get("model", {})))
138

    n_kv_heads: int = 0  # 0 = same as n_heads; fewer = grouped-query attention

    dense: bool = False  # DenseFormer: after each loop, a learned mix of all earlier loop outputs

    nh, hd = blk.n_heads, blk.head_dim
    h = F.rms_norm(x, (d,)) * g1
    q = F.linear(h, blk.q.weight).view(b, length, nh, hd).transpose(1, 2)
    k = F.linear(h, blk.k.weight).view(b, length, nh, hd).transpose(1, 2)

    """One application of a (shared) block; a plain function so per-loop tensors can be passed in."""

    b, length, d = x.shape
    nh, nkv, hd = blk.n_heads, blk.n_kv, blk.head_dim
    h = F.rms_norm(x, (d,)) * g1
    q = F.linear(h, blk.q.weight).view(b, length, nh, hd).transpose(1, 2)
    k = F.linear(h, blk.k.weight).view(b, length, nkv, hd).transpose(1, 2)
    v = F.linear(h, blk.v.weight)
    if ve is not None:
        v = v + ve
    v = v.view(b, length, nkv, hd).transpose(1, 2)
    if nkv != nh:
        k = k.repeat_interleave(nh // nkv, dim=1)
        v = v.repeat_interleave(nh // nkv, dim=1)
''')
s = s.replace('''        self.g_out = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(d))
''', '''        self.g_out = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(d))
        if cfg.ve:
            self.ve_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(depth, 1))
        if cfg.dense:
            # mix[l, j]: weight of loop-output j (0 = embedding) in the stream after loop l
            mix = torch.zeros(cfg.n_loops, cfg.n_loops + 1)
            mix[torch.arange(cfg.n_loops), torch.arange(1, cfg.n_loops + 1)] = 1.0
            self.mix = nn.Parameter(mix)
''')
s = s.replace('''        x = e
        i = 0
        for loop in range(cfg.n_loops):
            if cfg.inject and loop > 0:
                x = x + self.inj[loop] * e
            for block in self.blocks:

''', '''        x = e
        outs = [e]
        i = 0
        for loop in range(cfg.n_loops):
            if cfg.inject and loop > 0:
                x = x + self.inj[loop] * e
            for block in self.blocks:
                ve = self.ve_scale[i] * e if cfg.ve else None
                x = self.block_fn(block, x, cos, sin, self.g1[i], self.g2[i], self.sa[i], self.sm[i], ve)
                i += 1
            if cfg.dense:
                outs.append(x)
                x = sum(self.mix[loop, j] * o for j, o in enumerate(outs))
        return F.rms_norm(x, (cfg.d_model,)) * self.g_out
''')
open(p, "w").write(s)

    mem_act: str = "softmax"

    if gh is not None:
        u = u * gh
    return x + sm * F.linear(u, blk.proj.weight)


def apply_mem(x, table, g, s, temp, act: str):
    """Key-value memory whose keys and values are the (exempt) token embedding table."""

    h = F.rms_norm(x, (x.shape[-1],)) * g
    a = F.linear(h, table) * temp
    a = torch.softmax(a.float(), dim=-1).to(x.dtype) if act == "softmax" else F.relu(a).square()
    return x + s * F.linear(a, table.t())
''')
s = s.replace('''        if cfg.ve:
            self.ve_scale''', '''        if cfg.hgain:
            self.gh = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(depth, cfg.mlp_width))
        if cfg.mem_loops:
            n_mem = len(cfg.mem_loops)
            self.mem_g = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(n_mem, d))
            self.mem_s = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(n_mem, 1))
            self.mem_t = nn.Parameter(torch.full((n_mem, 1), 1.0))
        if cfg.ve:
            self.ve_scale''')
s = s.replace('''                ve = self.ve_scale[i] * e if cfg.ve else None
                x = self.block_fn(block, x, cos, sin, self.g1[i], self.g2[i], self.sa[i], self.sm[i], ve)
                i += 1
''', '''                ve = self.ve_scale[i] * e if cfg.ve else None
                gh = self.gh[i] if cfg.hgain else None
                x = self.block_fn(block, x, cos, sin, self.g1[i], self.g2[i], self.sa[i], self.sm[i], ve, gh)
                i += 1
            if loop in cfg.mem_loops:
                m = list(cfg.mem_loops).index(loop)
                x = self.mem_fn(x, self.embed.weight, self.mem_g[m], self.mem_s[m], self.mem_t[m], cfg.mem_act)
''')
s = s.replace('''        self.block_fn = apply_block  # train.py may replace with a compiled version
''', '''        self.block_fn = apply_block  # train.py may replace with a compiled version
        self.mem_fn = apply_mem
''')
open(p, "w").write(s)
EOF

def train(ctx):
    cfg = ctx.config["train"]
    model, budget = ctx.model, ctx.budget
    body = model.inner.body
    if cfg.get("compile", True) and ctx.device.type == "cuda":
        body.block_fn = torch.compile(body.block_fn)
        body.mem_fn = torch.compile(body.mem_fn)

    named = list(body.named_parameters())
    matrices = [p for n, p in named if p.dim() == 2 and n.startswith("blocks.")]
    embed = [body.embed.weight]

def train(ctx):
    cfg = ctx.config["train"]
    model, budget = ctx.model, ctx.budget
    body = model.inner.body

    embed = [body.embed.weight]

#40 Bashpython - <<'EOF'

p = "submission/model.py"
s = open(p).read()
old = s[s.index("            # mix[l, j]: weight"):s.index("            self.mix = nn.Parameter(mix)")]
s = s.replace(old, '''            # weights of loop-outputs 0..l+1 (0 = embedding) in the stream after loop l, stored flat
            self.mix_off = [sum(m + 2 for m in range(l)) for l in range(cfg.n_loops + 1)]
            mix = torch.zeros(self.mix_off[-1], d if cfg.dense_vec else 1)
            for l in range(cfg.n_loops):
                mix[self.mix_off[l] + l + 1] = 1.0
''')

              '''                w = self.mix[self.mix_off[loop]:self.mix_off[loop + 1]]
                x = sum(w[j] * o for j, o in enumerate(outs))''')
open(p, "w").write(s)

#43 Bashpython - <<'EOF'
python - <<'EOF'
p = "submission/model.py"
s = open(p).read()
s = s.replace('''    dense_vec: bool = False''', '''    ve_vec: bool = False  # per-channel value-embedding scales
    ke: bool = False  # add a per-application multiple of the token embedding to the attention keys
    smear: bool = False  # mix the previous token's embedding into each position's embedding (learned gate)
    dense_vec: bool = False''')
s = s.replace('''def apply_block(blk: Block, x, cos, sin, g1, g2, sa, sm, ve=None, gh=None):''',
              '''def apply_block(blk: Block, x, cos, sin, g1, g2, sa, sm, ve=None, gh=None, ke=None):''')
s = s.replace('''    k = F.linear(h, blk.k.weight).view(b, length, nkv, hd).transpose(1, 2)
''', '''    k = F.linear(h, blk.k.weight)
    if ke is not None:
        k = k + ke
    k = k.view(b, length, nkv, hd).transpose(1, 2)
''')
s = s.replace('''        if cfg.ve:
            self.ve_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(depth, 1))''', '''        if cfg.ve:
            self.ve_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(depth, d if cfg.ve_vec else 1))
        if cfg.ke:
            self.ke_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(depth, 1))
        if cfg.smear:
            self.smear_gate = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(d))''')
s = s.replace('''        e = F.rms_norm(self.embed(input_ids), (cfg.d_model,))
''', '''        e = self.embed(input_ids)
        if cfg.smear:
            prev = torch.cat((torch.zeros_like(e[:, :1]), e[:, :-1]), dim=1)
            e = e + self.smear_gate * prev
        e = F.rms_norm(e, (cfg.d_model,))
''')
s = s.replace('''                gh = self.gh[i] if cfg.hgain else None
                x = self.block_fn(block, x, cos, sin, self.g1[i], self.g2[i], self.sa[i], self.sm[i], ve, gh)''',
'''                gh = self.gh[i] if cfg.hgain else None
                ke = self.ke_scale[i] * e if cfg.ke else None
                x = self.block_fn(block, x, cos, sin, self.g1[i], self.g2[i], self.sa[i], self.sm[i], ve, gh, ke)''')
open(p, "w").write(s)

While that batch runs, adding a learnable per-head attention temperature (QK-norm caps attention logits at √64=8, which may limit sharp copying attention):

Ran 3 commandsBash ×3 (12s)
#45 Bashpython - <<'EOF'
python - <<'EOF'
p = "submission/model.py"
s = open(p).read()
s = s.replace('''    ve_vec: bool = False''', '''    qgain: bool = False  # learnable per-application, per-head attention temperature (with qk_norm)
    ve_vec: bool = False''')
s = s.replace('''def apply_block(blk: Block, x, cos, sin, g1, g2, sa, sm, ve=None, gh=None, ke=None):''',
              '''def apply_block(blk: Block, x, cos, sin, g1, g2, sa, sm, ve=None, gh=None, ke=None, qg=None):''')
s = s.replace('''        q = F.rms_norm(q, (hd,))
        k = F.rms_norm(k, (hd,))
''', '''        q = F.rms_norm(q, (hd,))
        k = F.rms_norm(k, (hd,))
        if qg is not None:
            q = q * qg.view(1, nh, 1, 1)
''')
s = s.replace('''        if cfg.ke:
            self.ke_scale''', '''        if cfg.qgain:
            self.qg = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(depth, cfg.n_heads))
        if cfg.ke:
            self.ke_scale''')
s = s.replace('''                ke = self.ke_scale[i] * e if cfg.ke else None
                x = self.block_fn(block, x, cos, sin, self.g1[i], self.g2[i], self.sa[i], self.sm[i], ve, gh, ke)''',
'''                ke = self.ke_scale[i] * e if cfg.ke else None
                qg = self.qg[i] if cfg.qgain else None
                x = self.block_fn(block, x, cos, sin, self.g1[i], self.g2[i], self.sa[i], self.sm[i], ve, gh, ke, qg)''')
open(p, "w").write(s)
EOF

#58 Bashpython - <<'EOF'
python - <<'EOF'
p = "submission/model.py"
s = open(p).read()
s = s.replace('''    qg_init: float = 1.0
''', '''    qg_init: float = 1.0
    me: bool = False  # add a per-application multiple of the token embedding to the MLP input
    attn_gate: bool = False  # head-wise sigmoid gate on the attention output, computed from the block input
''')
s = s.replace('''def apply_block(blk: Block, x, cos, sin, g1, g2, sa, sm, ve=None, gh=None, ke=None, qg=None):''',
              '''def apply_block(blk: Block, x, cos, sin, g1, g2, sa, sm, ve=None, gh=None, ke=None, qg=None, me=None):''')
s = s.replace('''    y = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
''', '''    y = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
    if blk.gate is not None:
        y = y * torch.sigmoid(F.linear(h, blk.gate.weight, blk.gate.bias)).transpose(1, 2)[..., None]
''')
s = s.replace('''    h = F.rms_norm(x, (d,)) * g2
''', '''    h = F.rms_norm(x, (d,)) * g2
    if me is not None:
        h = h + me
''')
s = s.replace('''        self.proj = nn.Linear(cfg.mlp_width, d, bias=False)
''', '''        self.proj = nn.Linear(cfg.mlp_width, d, bias=False)
        self.gate = nn.Linear(d, cfg.n_heads) if cfg.attn_gate else None
''')
s = s.replace('''        if cfg.ke:
            self.ke_scale''', '''        if cfg.me:
            self.me_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(depth, 1))
        if cfg.ke:
            self.ke_scale''')
s = s.replace('''                qg = self.qg[i] if cfg.qgain else None
                x = self.block_fn(block, x, cos, sin, self.g1[i], self.g2[i], self.sa[i], self.sm[i], ve, gh, ke, qg)''',
'''                qg = self.qg[i] if cfg.qgain else None
                me = self.me_scale[i] * e if cfg.me else None
                x = self.block_fn(block, x, cos, sin, self.g1[i], self.g2[i], self.sa[i], self.sm[i], ve, gh, ke, qg,
                                  me)''')
# gate init: zero weights, bias +2 -> gate ~0.88 initially
s = s.replace('''            for lin in (block.o, block.proj):''', '''            if block.gate is not None:
                nn.init.zeros_(block.gate.weight)
                nn.init.constant_(block.gate.bias, 2.0)
            for lin in (block.o, block.proj):''')
open(p, "w").write(s)

#62 Bashpython - <<'EOF'

p = "submission/model.py"
s = open(p).read()
s = s.replace('''    attn_gate: bool = False  # head-wise sigmoid gate on the attention output, computed from the block input
''', '''    attn_gate: bool = False  # sigmoid gate on the attention output, computed from the block input
    gate_groups: int = 0  # channels of the attention output share a gate in groups (0 = one gate per head)
''')
s = s.replace('''    y = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
    if blk.gate is not None:
        y = y * torch.sigmoid(F.linear(h, blk.gate.weight, blk.gate.bias)).transpose(1, 2)[..., None]
    x = x + sa * F.linear(y.transpose(1, 2).reshape(b, length, d), blk.o.weight)
''', '''    y = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True).transpose(1, 2)
    if blk.gate is not None:
        gate = torch.sigmoid(F.linear(h, blk.gate.weight, blk.gate.bias))
        y = (y.reshape(b, length, gate.shape[-1], -1) * gate[..., None])
    x = x + sa * F.linear(y.reshape(b, length, d), blk.o.weight)
''')
s = s.replace('''        self.gate = nn.Linear(d, cfg.n_heads) if cfg.attn_gate else None''',
              '''        self.gate = nn.Linear(d, cfg.gate_groups or cfg.n_heads) if cfg.attn_gate else None''')
open(p, "w").write(s)

p = "submission/config.json"
c = json.load(open(p)); c["model"]["attn_gate"] = True; c["train"]["embed_lr"] = 0.009; json.dump(c, open(p, "w"), indent=2)

#72 Read/workspace/submission/train.py
58	
59	
60	def train(ctx):
61	    cfg = ctx.config["train"]
62	    model, budget = ctx.model, ctx.budget
63	    body = model.inner.body
64	    if cfg.get("compile", True) and ctx.device.type == "cuda":
65	        if cfg.get("compile_full", False):
66	            body.forward = torch.compile(body.forward)
67	        else:
68	            body.block_fn = torch.compile(body.block_fn)
69	            body.mem_fn = torch.compile(body.mem_fn)
70	
71	    named = list(body.named_parameters())
72	    matrices = [p for n, p in named if p.dim() == 2 and n.startswith("blocks.")]
73	    embed = [body.embed.weight]
74	    others = [p for n, p in named if not (p.dim() == 2 and n.startswith("blocks.")) and p is not body.embed.weight]
75	    muon = Muon(matrices, lr=cfg["muon_lr"], momentum=cfg.get("muon_momentum", 0.95),
76	                weight_decay=cfg.get("muon_wd", 0.0))
77	    adam = torch.optim.AdamW([
78	        {"params": embed, "lr": cfg["embed_lr"], "weight_decay": cfg.get("embed_wd", 0.0)},
79	        {"params": others, "lr": cfg["scalar_lr"], "weight_decay": 0.0},
80	    ], betas=(cfg.get("beta1", 0.9), cfg.get("beta2", 0.95)), eps=1e-10, fused=ctx.device.type == "cuda")
81	    for opt in (muon, adam):
82	        for group in opt.param_groups:
83	            group["base_lr"] = group["lr"]
84	
85	    warmup, decay, final = cfg.get("warmup", 0.01), cfg.get("decay", 0.4), cfg.get("final_lr", 0.0)
86	
87	    def lr_scale(progress: float) -> float:
88	        if progress < warmup:
89	            return max(progress, 1e-3) / warmup
90	        if progress < 1 - decay:
91	            return 1.0
92	        p = (progress - (1 - decay)) / decay
93	        return final + (1 - final) * max(0.0, 1 - p)
94	
95	    params = [p for _, p in named]
96	    update = 0
97	    model.train()
98	    accumulation, micro = cfg["accumulation"], cfg["micro_batch"]
99	    clip = cfg.get("clip", 1.0)
100	    while not budget.exhausted:
101	        s = lr_scale(budget.fraction_used)
102	        for opt in (muon, adam):
103	            for group in opt.param_groups:
104	                group["lr"] = group["base_lr"] * s
105	        # Muon momentum warmup
106	        if cfg.get("momentum_warmup", 0):
107	            frac = min(1.0, update / cfg["momentum_warmup"])
108	            for group in muon.param_groups:
109	                group["momentum"] = (1 - frac) * 0.85 + frac * cfg.get("muon_momentum", 0.95)
110	        total = 0.0
111	        for _ in range(accumulation):
112	            batch = ctx.data.sample(micro)
113	            with torch.autocast(ctx.device.type, dtype=torch.bfloat16):
114	                logits = model(batch.tokens)
115	            loss = F.cross_entropy(logits.float().flatten(0, 1), batch.targets.flatten())
116	            (loss / accumulation).backward()
117	            total += loss.detach() / accumulation
118	        if clip:
119	            torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params, clip)
120	        muon.step()
121	        adam.step()
122	        muon.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
123	        adam.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
124	        update += 1
125	        if update % cfg["log_every"] == 0:
126	            total = total.item()
127	            if not math.isfinite(total):

p = "submission/train.py"
s = open(p).read()
s = s.replace('''import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
''', '''import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F

from lmenv.api import BudgetExhausted
''')
s = s.replace('''    body = model.inner.body
    if cfg.get("compile", True) and ctx.device.type == "cuda":
        if cfg.get("compile_full", False):
            body.forward = torch.compile(body.forward)
        else:
            body.block_fn = torch.compile(body.block_fn)
            body.mem_fn = torch.compile(body.mem_fn)
''', '''    body = model.inner.body
    eager_fns = (body.block_fn, body.mem_fn)
    compiled = cfg.get("compile", True) and ctx.device.type == "cuda"
    if compiled:
        if cfg.get("compile_full", False):
            body.forward = torch.compile(body.forward)
        else:
            body.block_fn = torch.compile(body.block_fn)
            body.mem_fn = torch.compile(body.mem_fn)

    def uncompile():
        if "forward" in vars(body):
            del body.forward
        body.block_fn, body.mem_fn = eager_fns
''')
s = s.replace('''        for _ in range(accumulation):
            batch = ctx.data.sample(micro)
            with torch.autocast(ctx.device.type, dtype=torch.bfloat16):
                logits = model(batch.tokens)
            loss = F.cross_entropy(logits.float().flatten(0, 1), batch.targets.flatten())
            (loss / accumulation).backward()
            total += loss.detach() / accumulation
''', '''        for _ in range(accumulation):
            batch = ctx.data.sample(micro)

            def step_loss():
                with torch.autocast(ctx.device.type, dtype=torch.bfloat16):
                    logits = model(batch.tokens)
                loss = F.cross_entropy(logits.float().flatten(0, 1), batch.targets.flatten())
                (loss / accumulation).backward()
                return loss.detach()

            if compiled and update == 0:
                try:
                    loss = step_loss()
                except BudgetExhausted:
                    raise
                except Exception as error:  # torch.compile unavailable: train uncompiled instead
                    ctx.log({"compile_failed": 1.0})
                    print(f"torch.compile failed ({type(error).__name__}: {error}); training uncompiled")
                    uncompile()
                    compiled = False
                    for p in params:
                        p.grad = None
                    loss = step_loss()
            else:
                loss = step_loss()
            total += loss / accumulation
''')
open(p, "w").write(s)

While those run, let me update the docstrings in the submission to describe the final method accurately.

Ran 2 commandsBash ×2 (12s)
#77 Bashpython - <<'EOF'
python - <<'EOF'
p = "submission/model.py"
s = open(p).read()
start = s.index('"""'); end = s.index('"""', 3) + 3
s = s[:start] + '''"""Looped (weight-shared) transformer: few unique weights, many FLOPs per token.

The token embedding (8192 x 512, exempt from the weight cap) feeds a residual
stream of width 512. One shared transformer block (attention + squared-ReLU
MLP of width 1792, ~2.9M weights) is applied n_loops = 16 times, which spends
~117M of the 120M FLOP cap per token. Cheap per-application parameters let the
shared block act differently at each depth:

* RMSNorm gains and residual-branch scales for every application;
* value embeddings (ve): every application adds a learned multiple of the
  normalised token embedding to the attention values, so token identity stays
  directly available at every depth;
* a learned per-head attention temperature (qgain) on top of QK-RMSNorm;
* a head-wise sigmoid gate on the attention output, computed from the block
  input (attn_gate);
* DenseFormer mixing (dense): after each loop the stream is a learned weighted
  sum of the embedding and all earlier loop outputs.

Attention uses RoPE and is causal. The model returns RMS-normed hidden states
(with a learned gain); the harness's tied output head turns them into logits.
Other options in ModelConfig were tried and are off by default.
"""''' + s[end:]
open(p, "w").write(s)
p = "submission/train.py"
s = open(p).read()
start = s.index('"""'); end = s.index('"""', 3) + 3
s = s[:start] + '''"""Training recipe: next-token loss, Muon for the shared block's matrices, Adam for the rest.

Random 512-token windows in batches of 16K tokens (32 x 512), bf16 autocast,
full-model torch.compile (falls back to eager if compilation fails). The block's
weight matrices are trained with Muon (Nesterov momentum 0.95, update
orthogonalised by a Newton-Schulz iteration, lr 0.02); the tied token
embedding (lr 0.013) and the per-loop gains/scales/mixing weights (lr 0.01) use
Adam. Learning rate: 1% linear warmup, constant, then linear decay to zero over
the last 40% of the budget. Training stops when the budget is spent.
"""''' + s[end:]
open(p, "w").write(s)

head -30 submission/model.py; python -m lmenv.run check | tail -1
"""Looped (weight-shared) transformer: few unique weights, many FLOPs per token.

The token embedding (8192 x 512, exempt from the weight cap) feeds a residual
stream of width 512. One shared transformer block (attention + squared-ReLU
MLP of width 1792, ~2.9M weights) is applied n_loops = 16 times, which spends
~117M of the 120M FLOP cap per token. Cheap per-application parameters let the
shared block act differently at each depth:

* RMSNorm gains and residual-branch scales for every application;
* value embeddings (ve): every application adds a learned multiple of the
  normalised token embedding to the attention values, so token identity stays
  directly available at every depth;
* a learned per-head attention temperature (qgain) on top of QK-RMSNorm;
* a head-wise sigmoid gate on the attention output, computed from the block
  input (attn_gate);
* DenseFormer mixing (dense): after each loop the stream is a learned weighted
  sum of the embedding and all earlier loop outputs.

Attention uses RoPE and is causal. The model returns RMS-normed hidden states
(with a learned gain); the harness's tied output head turns them into logits.
Other options in ModelConfig were tried and are off by default.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass

import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F

"""Training recipe: next-token loss, Muon for the shared block's matrices, Adam for the rest.

Random 512-token windows in batches of 24K tokens (48 x 512), bf16 autocast,
full-model torch.compile (falls back to eager if compilation fails). The block's
weight matrices are trained with Muon (Nesterov momentum 0.95, update
orthogonalised by a Newton-Schulz iteration, lr 0.02); the tied token
embedding (lr 0.02) and the per-loop gains/scales/mixing weights (lr 0.01) use
Adam. Learning rate: 1% linear warmup, constant, then linear decay to zero over
the last 40% of the budget. Training stops when the budget is spent.
"""

    "inject": true,
    "ve": true,
    "dense": true,
    "qgain": true,
    "attn_gate": true
  },
  "train": {
    "muon_lr": 0.02,
    "muon_momentum": 0.95,
    "embed_lr": 0.02,
    "scalar_lr": 0.01,
    "micro_batch": 48,
    "accumulation": 1,
    "warmup": 0.01,
    "decay": 0.4,
    "final_lr": 0.0,
    "clip": 1.0,
    "compile": true,
    "log_every": 100,
    "compile_full": true
  }
}same-config-as-combo

#93
3h 37m

The final submission scores 3.372 nats on the dev validation text, averaged over three full-budget seeds. The starting point scores 3.644, so that is 0.272 nats lower (1.25 vs 1.35 bits per byte). It passes lmenv.run check: 2,968,412 counted weights (cap 3M), 119.6M FLOPs per token (cap 120M), deterministic, and nothing flagged by the code scan. No jobs are left running.

Model. One shared transformer block, 512 wide, is applied 16 times in a row. It has 4 attention heads, QK-norm, RoPE and a squared-ReLU MLP of width 1856. Reusing the block is what turns the spare compute into depth without adding weights. The token embedding is the full 512 wide, since that table doesn't count against the cap. Each of the 16 applications gets its own cheap settings, a few thousand weights in total.

Training. Muon for the block's weight matrices (learning rate 0.02), Adam for the embedding (0.02) and the per-step settings (0.01). Batches of 24K tokens. Short warmup, constant learning rate, then a linear decay to zero over the last 40%. One run takes about 7 minutes on the H200. If torch.compile fails on your machine, training falls back to running uncompiled, which is slower but still completes; I tested this with a simulated failure.

What helped (each confirmed in a full-budget run):

StepFull-run loss
Shared block looped 16×, 512-wide embedding, Muon3.456
+ token embedding added to the attention values at every step (biggest single gain) + learned mixing of earlier steps' outputs3.427
+ learned attention sharpness per head3.413
16K-token batches instead of 32K3.393 (2 seeds)
+ gate on each head's attention output, higher embedding learning rate3.376 (2 seeds)
4 heads of 128 instead of 8 of 643.371 (3 seeds)

What didn't help in shorter 50M-token test runs: grouped-query attention, two smaller shared blocks, reusing the embedding table as a memory layer, per-channel scales, mixing in the previous token's embedding, 16 heads, and 64K-token batches. Gains measured in those short runs carried over to full runs fairly well, except the gate plus higher learning rate, which gained only about a third as much at full length.

The last step is a tie. Widening the MLP to 1856, raising the embedding learning rate to 0.02 and using 24K batches each looked better in a single full run. Together, over three seeds, they average 3.372, the same as the previous version's 3.371. I kept the combined version because it is the one run end to end from submission/, it uses the full weight budget, and it trains faster. Expect about ±0.01 from seed alone, and the score on your own texts may differ from these dev numbers.

Snapshots 001–008 record each step with its runs; 006 is the tied previous version and 008 matches what is in submission/.
